# PE-PROTOCOL-SPECIFICATION-G4 — unpublished composition candidate

Version 4.0.0. Candidate source generation PE-PROTOCOL-GENERATION-004 is not published. This is not a completed published Protocol Specification or a conformance basis.

All 50 inherited requirement families in 16 section families are composed below. The 861 inherited requirement meanings are preserved exactly, with successor requirement/source identifiers and historical supporting references. The 65 additional candidate sentence homes include scoped qualifications and two optional-adapter representation duties. All 97 reviewed sentence units are accounted for in the delta disposition register; restatements and explanatory units do not manufacture new obligations. Formal source declarations and all admission/privacy requirements remain applicable.

The boundary is incomplete profile/materiality/privacy construction and absent independent appointment/admission, as precisely recorded in acceptance-defects.tsv. Neither 941 homes nor author checks establish normative admission or summit completion.

## SF-01 / RF-01 — Specification identity, derivation, completeness, and authority

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-001"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Specification | A Protocol Specification MUST possess one stable specification identity and MUST identify its specification version. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-002"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-002 | MUST | applicable | Every completed Privacy Engine Protocol Specification | A completed Protocol Specification MUST identify exactly one published Privacy Engine protocol generation from which it derives. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-003"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Specification | A Protocol Specification MUST preserve the semantics and obligations of the published protocol generation from which it derives. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-004"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-004 | MUST | applicable | Every completed Privacy Engine Protocol Specification | A completed Protocol Specification MUST contain the complete implementation-facing normative composition required to implement its identified published protocol generation without dependence on hidden repository context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-005"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete cumulative Protocol Specification | An incomplete cumulative Protocol Specification MUST NOT claim completeness, final publication status, or suitability as the complete conformance basis. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-006"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Specification and every interpretation of it | A Protocol Specification MUST NOT be treated as independent protocol authority or as authority to modify its source protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-007"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-007 | MUST | applicable | Every cumulative Protocol Specification | A cumulative Protocol Specification MUST state its composition status, composed section families, deferred section families, and current completeness boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-008"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-008 | MUST | applicable | Every semantic requirement composed into the Protocol Specification | Each semantic requirement MUST possess exactly one authoritative normative home within the Protocol Specification, excluding every supporting reference from authoritative-home status. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-spec-009"></a> PE-G4-SPEC-009 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation profile | The Protocol Specification MUST establish the downstream derivation rule requiring the implementation profile to identify the Protocol Specification and published Protocol Generation from which its applicable obligations derive. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10 | NONE |

## SF-01 / RF-02 — Protocol definition, scope, and invariant ownership

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-prot-001"></a> PE-G4-PROT-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol definition | The Privacy Engine Protocol MUST be defined independently of any particular implementation, application, deployment, transport, storage system, or execution environment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-prot-002"></a> PE-G4-PROT-002 | MUST | applicable | Every published Privacy Engine protocol generation | A published protocol generation MUST declare the complete bounded protocol scope represented by that generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-prot-003"></a> PE-G4-PROT-003 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol invariant | Each protocol invariant MUST remain owned by the published protocol generation in which it is defined. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-prot-004"></a> PE-G4-PROT-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream specification, profile, implementation, application, assessment, and certification artifact | A downstream artifact MUST NOT redefine, weaken, strengthen, omit, or silently reinterpret an applicable protocol invariant. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-prot-005"></a> PE-G4-PROT-005 | MUST | applicable | Every claim of complete protocol realization | A claim of complete protocol realization MUST account for every applicable invariant and normative requirement of the identified published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S09 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-prot-006"></a> PE-G4-PROT-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application and implementation using the Privacy Engine Protocol | Application-defined meaning and implementation-defined mechanism MUST NOT be represented as protocol-defined meaning unless the identified published protocol generation explicitly defines it. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |

## SF-01 / RF-05 — Normative language, interpretation, applicability, and strength

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-001"></a> PE-G4-NORM-001 | MUST | applicable | Every statement in the Protocol Specification | Every specification statement MUST be classifiable as normative or informative. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-002"></a> PE-G4-NORM-002 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement using `MUST` | A requirement using `MUST` MUST be interpreted as mandatory throughout its declared applicability scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-003"></a> PE-G4-NORM-003 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement using `MUST NOT` | A requirement using `MUST NOT` MUST be interpreted as prohibiting the stated behavior throughout its declared applicability scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-004"></a> PE-G4-NORM-004 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement using `SHOULD` | A requirement using `SHOULD` MUST be interpreted as recommended; deviation MUST be bounded, understood, and justified without violating any mandatory requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-005"></a> PE-G4-NORM-005 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement using `SHOULD NOT` | A requirement using `SHOULD NOT` MUST be interpreted as discouraged; contrary behavior MUST be bounded, understood, and justified without violating any mandatory requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-006"></a> PE-G4-NORM-006 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement using `MAY` | A requirement using `MAY` MUST be interpreted as permitting, but not requiring, the stated behavior. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-007"></a> PE-G4-NORM-007 | MUST | applicable | Every declaration of implementation-defined behavior | Implementation-defined behavior MUST be explicitly permitted, bounded by the applicable protocol requirements, and documented by the implementation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-008"></a> PE-G4-NORM-008 | MUST | applicable | Every normative requirement | Every normative requirement MUST declare a bounded applicability rule identifying the subjects and conditions to which it applies. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-009"></a> PE-G4-NORM-009 | MUST | applicable | Every interpretation of a normative requirement | A normative requirement MUST be interpreted using its identifier, requirement text, defined terminology, applicability rule, authoritative section, and applicable cross-references as one coherent requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-010"></a> PE-G4-NORM-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every supporting cross-reference and informative statement | A supporting cross-reference or informative statement MUST NOT independently create, modify, complete, weaken, strengthen, conceal, or relocate a normative obligation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-011"></a> PE-G4-NORM-011 | MUST | applicable | Every normative requirement | Every normative requirement MUST possess exactly one stable identifier in the form `PE-<SEMANTIC-FAMILY>-<NNN>`, and that identifier MUST denote exactly one semantic requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-012"></a> PE-G4-NORM-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every requirement identifier lifecycle | A requirement identifier MUST NOT be changed because of editorial reordering and MUST NOT be silently reused for a different semantic requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-013"></a> PE-G4-NORM-013 | MUST | applicable | Every normative requirement | Every normative requirement MUST record its requirement family, strength, applicability, authoritative section, and supporting source traceability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-norm-014"></a> PE-G4-NORM-014 | MUST | applicable | Every apparently obligatory specification statement | Every statement intended to impose an obligation MUST be expressed as a stable identified normative requirement rather than apparently mandatory prose. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05 | NONE |

## SF-02 / RF-03 — Protocol generation, publication, and Published Protocol

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-001"></a> PE-G4-GEN-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Generation | A Protocol Generation MUST possess one unique and stable generation identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-002"></a> PE-G4-GEN-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Generation | A Protocol Generation MUST contain one bounded and complete protocol semantic state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-003"></a> PE-G4-GEN-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete protocol fragment or partial change | An incomplete protocol fragment or partial change MUST NOT be represented as a complete Protocol Generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S11 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-004"></a> PE-G4-GEN-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Generation lifecycle | A Protocol Generation MUST retain the same identity through publication, coexistence, deprecation, migration, and retirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-005"></a> PE-G4-GEN-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Publication | A Protocol Publication MUST identify exactly one complete Protocol Generation as its publication subject. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-006"></a> PE-G4-GEN-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Publication | A Protocol Publication MUST identify the complete published material, generation identity, publication identity, and publication version required to distinguish its result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-007"></a> PE-G4-GEN-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete or ambiguous publication occurrence | An incomplete or ambiguously identified publication MUST NOT establish a Published Protocol. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-008"></a> PE-G4-GEN-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Publication | A Protocol Publication MUST NOT be interpreted as Protocol Succession, Protocol Lineage membership, compatibility, deprecation, migration, or retirement unless those relationships are separately and explicitly established. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-009"></a> PE-G4-GEN-009 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Protocol | A Published Protocol MUST identify exactly one Protocol Generation and exactly one complete publication of that generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-010"></a> PE-G4-GEN-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Protocol | A Published Protocol MUST possess a stable identity distinct from the identity of its Protocol Generation and its Protocol Publication. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-011"></a> PE-G4-GEN-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Published Protocol | A Published Protocol MUST NOT be silently replaced, partially rewritten, or reinterpreted as a different generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-012"></a> PE-G4-GEN-012 | MUST | applicable | Every published Privacy Engine protocol generation | Each published Privacy Engine protocol generation MUST remain the protocol authority for its own identified semantics and obligations throughout its recorded lifecycle. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-013"></a> PE-G4-GEN-013 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Publication claiming completeness | A Protocol Publication claiming completeness MUST contain or unambiguously bind every component required to reconstruct the complete published generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-014"></a> PE-G4-GEN-014 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Protocol | A Published Protocol MUST remain immutable after publication. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-015"></a> PE-G4-GEN-015 | MUST | applicable | Every correction, clarification, or replacement affecting published material | A correction, clarification, or replacement affecting published material MUST be separately identified and MUST preserve the identity and historical content of the earlier Published Protocol. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-016"></a> PE-G4-GEN-016 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete publication | An incomplete publication MUST NOT be used as the authoritative representation of a complete Protocol Generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-017"></a> PE-G4-GEN-017 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine implementation | An implementation MUST identify exactly which published Protocol Generation it targets. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-018"></a> PE-G4-GEN-018 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance determination | A conformance determination MUST identify the implementation, implementation profile, Protocol Specification, and published Protocol Generation against which conformance is assessed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-019"></a> PE-G4-GEN-019 | MUST | applicable | Every certification | A certification MUST identify the conformance basis and published Protocol Generation to which the certification applies. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-gen-020"></a> PE-G4-GEN-020 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream implementation or assessment artifact | A downstream implementation, profile, conformance determination, or certification MUST NOT modify the identity, semantics, obligations, lifecycle, or authority of its referenced published Protocol Generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |

## SF-02 / RF-04 — Succession, lineage, versioning, deprecation, and migration

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-001"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Succession relationship | A Protocol Succession relationship MUST identify exactly one predecessor Protocol Generation and one distinct successor Protocol Generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-002"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-002 | MUST | applicable | Every successor Protocol Generation | A successor Protocol Generation MUST possess an identity distinct from every predecessor generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-003"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Succession relationship | Protocol Succession MUST preserve the identity, published content, lifecycle record, and historical truth of the predecessor generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-004"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Succession relationship | Protocol Succession MUST NOT mutate, rename, merge, delete, or retroactively reinterpret its predecessor generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-005"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Publication | Publication of a generation MUST NOT by itself establish succession from any other generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S11, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-006"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Lineage | A Protocol Lineage MUST possess one stable lineage identity distinct from the identities of its member generations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S14 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-007"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Lineage membership claim | A Protocol Generation MUST be included in a lineage only through an explicit lineage-membership rule supported by the applicable succession structure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-008"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Lineage | A Protocol Lineage MUST preserve the separate identities and lifecycle records of all member generations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S14 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-009"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Lineage | A Protocol Lineage MUST NOT create, inherit, transfer, or replace the protocol authority of any member generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-010"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Lineage model | A Protocol Lineage model MUST permit independent Protocol Generations to remain in separate Protocol Lineages and MUST require an explicit succession relationship before placing generations in one lineage. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-011"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Lineage membership claim | Lineage membership MUST NOT by itself be interpreted as compatibility, migration eligibility, deprecation, replacement, or semantic equivalence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-012"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-012 | MUST | applicable | Every published Protocol Generation | Every published Protocol Generation MUST possess a stable version designation sufficient to distinguish it from every other published generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-013"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every protocol version designation | A version designation MUST NOT by itself be interpreted as proof of compatibility, incompatibility, succession, migration eligibility, or semantic equivalence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-014"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-014 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol semantic change | A change to protocol-defined semantics, obligations, validity, invariants, or permitted behavior MUST be represented by a distinct Protocol Generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-015"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every published Protocol Generation | Semantic change MUST NOT be introduced through silent mutation, editorial replacement, implementation variation, or reinterpretation of an existing Published Protocol. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-016"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-016 | MUST | applicable | Every classification of a protocol change | A protocol change MUST be classified according to its semantic effect rather than only its label, release number, implementation impact, or publication format. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-017"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-017 | MUST | applicable | Every claim of compatible evolution | A claim of compatible evolution MUST identify the generations, requirements, subjects, and compatibility scope to which the claim applies. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-018"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-018 | MAY | applicable | Every implementation variation within one published generation | An implementation variation MAY occur without a new Protocol Generation only when every applicable protocol semantic and normative requirement remains preserved. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-019"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-019 | MUST | applicable | Every compatible successor generation | A compatible successor generation MUST remain distinctly identified and MUST preserve every predecessor obligation included within the declared compatibility scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-020"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-020 | MUST | applicable | Every breaking protocol evolution | Breaking evolution MUST be represented by a distinct successor generation and MUST explicitly identify the affected compatibility boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-021"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-021 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every compatibility claim | Compatibility MUST NOT be used to merge generation identities, mutate historical published truth, or conceal semantic change. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-022"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-022 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Generation deprecation | Deprecation MUST identify the deprecated generation, the effective lifecycle condition, and any published continuation or migration guidance. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-023"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-023 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Generation deprecation | Deprecation MUST NOT revoke, erase, mutate, or retroactively invalidate the identity, published content, or historical truth of the deprecated generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-024"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-024 | MUST | applicable | Every published migration | A published migration MUST identify its source target, destination target, applicability conditions, required steps, preserved properties, and explicit failure conditions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-025"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-025 | MUST | applicable | Every mandatory migration obligation | A mandatory migration obligation MUST be expressed as an identified normative requirement with explicit applicability and requirement strength. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-026"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-026 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every migration | Migration MUST NOT mutate the identity of a Protocol Generation, Published Protocol, implementation target, conformance determination, or certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-027"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-027 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every deprecation or migration statement | A deprecation or migration statement MUST NOT silently redefine compatibility, succession, lineage membership, or protocol semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-028"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-028 | MUST | applicable | Every environment supporting multiple published Protocol Generations | Coexisting Protocol Generations MUST remain separately identifiable and MUST retain separate applicability, compatibility, lifecycle, and authority records. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-029"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-029 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every coexistence arrangement | Coexistence MUST NOT be interpreted as semantic equivalence, automatic compatibility, shared identity, succession, or migration eligibility. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-030"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-030 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Generation retirement | Retirement MUST identify the retired generation, retirement scope, effective condition, and consequences for future implementation, assessment, and support. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-031"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-031 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Generation retirement | Retirement MUST NOT delete, mutate, conceal, or retroactively invalidate the retired generation or its historical protocol truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-evol-032"></a> PE-G4-EVOL-032 | MUST | applicable | Every lifecycle interpretation | Deprecation, migration, coexistence, and retirement MUST be interpreted as distinct bounded lifecycle concepts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S16 | NONE |

## SF-03 / RF-06 — Protocol terminology

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-term-001"></a> PE-G4-TERM-001 | MUST | applicable | Every use of a canonical Protocol Specification term | A canonical term MUST be interpreted according to its definition and bounded role in this specification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-term-002"></a> PE-G4-TERM-002 | MUST | applicable | Every downstream section, profile, implementation, application adapter, assessment, and supporting artifact | Every downstream use of a canonical term MUST preserve the same subject, relationship, scope, and identity distinction established by this specification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-term-003"></a> PE-G4-TERM-003 | MUST | applicable | Every term that may denote either a protocol type or an occurrence | A term that may denote either a type or an occurrence MUST explicitly preserve the applicable type-or-occurrence distinction. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-term-004"></a> PE-G4-TERM-004 | MUST | applicable | Every specification definition involving a semantic subject, identifier, or representation | The terminology used for a semantic subject, its identifier, and its representation MUST preserve those as distinct concepts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-term-005"></a> PE-G4-TERM-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every alias, abbreviation, implementation name, application label, and editorial synonym | An alias, abbreviation, implementation name, application label, or editorial synonym MUST NOT silently redefine, merge, widen, or narrow a canonical protocol term. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-term-006"></a> PE-G4-TERM-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every terminology decision and editorial reorganization | Terminology or editorial structure MUST NOT introduce a new first-class protocol subject, lifecycle, authority, or semantic boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-term-007"></a> PE-G4-TERM-007 | MUST | applicable | Every permitted local alias for a canonical protocol term | A permitted local alias MUST map explicitly and unambiguously to exactly one canonical term while preserving its protocol meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |

## SF-03 / RF-07 — Protocol subject identity and identity separation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-001"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-001 | MUST | applicable | Every established identified protocol subject | Every established identified protocol subject MUST retain its own bounded identity according to its defined architectural role. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-002"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action, Protocol Action Evaluation, Protocol Action Determination, and Protocol State Transition | Protocol Action, Evaluation, Determination, and Protocol State Transition identities MUST remain mutually distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-003"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation and its Protocol Action Determination | Evaluation and Determination MUST retain distinct process and result identities while remaining two bounded aspects of one architectural model. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-004"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Operation and associated Protocol Action | A Protocol Operation capability and each Protocol Action occurrence classified by it MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-005"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Transition and governed Protocol State Transition | A Protocol Transition mapping and each Protocol State Transition occurrence governed by it MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-006"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-006 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between identified protocol subjects | Every subject relationship MUST preserve its declared direction, bounded meaning, and participating subject identities. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-007"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-007 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol subject referenced through an implementation, application, runtime mechanism, or application interpretation | A protocol subject MUST remain distinct from the implementation, application, runtime mechanism, and application-defined meaning through which it is represented or used. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-008"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-008 | MUST | applicable | Every downstream representation or reference to an established protocol subject | Every downstream representation or reference MUST preserve the identity of the same established protocol subject rather than substitute a newly inferred subject. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-009"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every relationship, composition, representation, and implementation convenience involving distinct subjects | A relationship, composition, representation, or implementation convenience MUST NOT silently merge distinct protocol subjects or their identities. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-subj-010"></a> PE-G4-SUBJ-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proposed extension to the established protocol-subject inventory | A proposed extension MUST NOT introduce a new first-class subject unless independent identity, meaning, semantic ownership, and irreducible lifecycle necessity are all established. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28 | NONE |

## SF-03 / RF-08 — Protocol identifiers and occurrence identifiers

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-id-001"></a> PE-G4-ID-001 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol identifier | Every protocol identifier MUST possess one declared bounded role identifying exactly the subject or occurrence specified by that role. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-002"></a> PE-G4-ID-002 | MUST | applicable | Every identified protocol subject and its identifier | A protocol subject and the identifier used to reference it MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-003"></a> PE-G4-ID-003 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol subject and its syntactic, serialized, stored, transmitted, or displayed representation | A semantic protocol subject and each representation of it MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-004"></a> PE-G4-ID-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every relationship between separately identified protocol subjects | The identifier of one related protocol subject MUST NOT be substituted for the identifier of another subject. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-005"></a> PE-G4-ID-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every identifier of a Protocol Action occurrence | A Protocol Action identifier MUST NOT identify, encode, imply, or reconstruct the Private Actor who performed the action. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-006"></a> PE-G4-ID-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public Evaluation, Determination, and Protocol State Transition identifier | An Evaluation, Determination, or Protocol State Transition identifier MUST NOT identify, encode, imply, or reconstruct a Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-007"></a> PE-G4-ID-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation, transaction, retry, transport-attempt, storage, or diagnostic identifier | An operational identifier MUST NOT silently become or redefine a protocol-subject or occurrence identifier. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-008"></a> PE-G4-ID-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every protocol identifier outside its declared subject and context | A protocol identifier MUST NOT be reused outside its declared role as a universal participant identifier, cross-subject join key, or cross-action identity mechanism. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-id-009"></a> PE-G4-ID-009 | MUST | applicable | Every permitted change of protocol-subject representation | A representation change MUST preserve the identity, semantics, relationships, and bounded identifier role of the represented protocol subject. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseid-001-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEID-001-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every registry, mapping, profile, authorization, U, comparison attempt, definition and recovery table MUST have one identity satisfying its declared grammar. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69, PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseid-001-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEID-001-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every registry, mapping, profile, authorization, U, comparison attempt, definition and recovery table MUST remain distinct from every other identity class. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69, PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseid-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEID-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every comparison attempt identity MUST be occurrence-local, contiguous within U and immutable with its result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-04 / RF-09 — Private actors and public protocol subjects

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-001"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine realization of a public protocol action | Every realization MUST preserve the architectural principle `Private actors, public actions`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-002"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-002 | MUST | applicable | Every publicly visible Protocol Action | A publicly visible Protocol Action MUST remain verifiable to its declared protocol extent without disclosure of the Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-003"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-003 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Private Actor and a public Protocol Action | The performer relationship MUST preserve the Private Actor and Protocol Action as distinct subjects. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-004"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-004 | MUST | applicable | Every publicly identified protocol subject | A public protocol-subject identifier MUST identify only its declared protocol subject and role. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-005"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-005 | MUST | applicable | Every public protocol consequence | A public protocol consequence MUST remain attributable to the applicable public protocol occurrence without becoming attributable to a disclosed Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-006"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Action validity, authorization, uniqueness, and public-consequence rule | A rule governing a public Protocol Action MUST NOT require disclosure of civil, wallet, enrollment, admission, application, implementation, deployment, or transport identity as Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-actsep-007"></a> PE-G4-ACTSEP-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public Privacy Engine protocol surface | A public protocol surface MUST NOT require or create a reusable public participant identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |

## SF-04 / RF-10 — Privacy, anonymity, and unlinkability

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-001"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine protocol action governed by anonymous-participation semantics | The protocol action MUST permit verification of its applicable validity properties without disclosure of the Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-002"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-002 | MUST | applicable | Every public value and public protocol subject exposed by an anonymous action | Private Actor identity MUST remain non-inferable from each exposed value or subject within the protocol-defined privacy boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-003"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-003 | MUST | applicable | Every pair or collection of anonymous Protocol Actions in distinct action contexts | Cross-action unlinkability MUST be preserved unless the published protocol generation expressly defines a narrower public relationship that remains non-identifying. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-004"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every wallet identifier, wallet signature, wallet-derived value, and wallet relationship | Wallet identity MUST NOT be substituted for, used to infer, or represented as anonymous membership identity or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-005"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application identifier, account, profile, metadata value, and application-defined relationship | Application identity or application metadata MUST NOT be substituted for, used to infer, or represented as protocol identity or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-006"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation identifier and implementation-specific internal value | Implementation identity or internal implementation context MUST NOT be substituted for, used to infer, or represented as protocol identity or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-007"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every deployment identifier, topology coordinate, service identity, and infrastructure relationship | Deployment identity or infrastructure topology MUST NOT be substituted for, used to infer, or represented as protocol identity or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-008"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transport identifier, connection attribute, delivery path, retry path, and request context | Transport identity or delivery context MUST NOT be substituted for, used to infer, or represented as protocol identity or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-009"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every enrollment right, admission object, funding relationship, sponsor relationship, and associated provenance | Enrollment, admission, funding, or sponsor provenance MUST NOT be carried into public-action identity or used as an action-layer correlation value. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-010"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-010 | MUST | applicable | Every action-local public value | An action-local public value MUST remain bounded to its declared action role, action context, and permitted protocol interpretation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-011"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-011 | MUST | applicable | Every privacy-sensitive failure path | Failure handling MUST preserve Private Actor non-disclosure and the non-identity-bearing role of public values. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-priv-012"></a> PE-G4-PRIV-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation, profile, application, conformance claim, diagnostic, and specification interpretation | A Privacy Engine artifact MUST NOT claim anonymity, unlinkability, metadata protection, or transport protection beyond the bounded guarantees of the identified published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every mapping profile MUST project only the minimum protocol-owned components required by T. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A projection, authorization or comparison MUST NOT contain Private Actor identity, admission or enrollment provenance, reusable participant identity or an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-003-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A blinded component opening MUST remain confidential. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-003-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A blinded component opening MUST be disclosed only inside an authorized comparison or independent privacy-validation boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-004"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Component identifiers, commitments and result identifiers MUST be assessed in composition for uniqueness, correlation and cross-action linkage before profile admission. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-005-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-005-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Failure, recovery, retry and observability surfaces MUST preserve Private actors, public actions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepriv-005-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRIV-005-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | Failure, recovery, retry and observability surfaces MUST NOT expand disclosure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-04 / RF-11 — Compositional privacy, observability, and forbidden correlation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-001"></a> PE-G4-COMP-001 | MUST | applicable | Every composition of publicly exposed Privacy Engine values, subjects, consequences, and records | The composition MUST remain non-identifying for the Private Actor within the protocol-defined privacy boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-002"></a> PE-G4-COMP-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation-controlled identifier, timing label, diagnostic, serialization field, log field, storage key, route, and public response | An implementation-controlled surface MUST NOT create an actor-reconstruction, provenance, or unauthorized cross-action correlation bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-003"></a> PE-G4-COMP-003 | MUST | applicable | Every privacy-sensitive field name, variable name, route name, log label, diagnostic, comment, example, test, serialization field, and specification term | Privacy-sensitive naming and documentation MUST use neutral terminology that preserves non-attribution and the bounded role of each value. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-004"></a> PE-G4-COMP-004 | MUST | applicable | Every public observability surface | Public observability MUST expose only the minimum role-bounded information required by the applicable protocol semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-005"></a> PE-G4-COMP-005 | MUST | applicable | Every diagnostic and failure-reporting surface | A diagnostic MUST describe only the applicable protocol subject, status, or bounded failure classification without identifying or reconstructing the Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-006"></a> PE-G4-COMP-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every request history, telemetry record, path-acquisition record, submission record, and operational history | Operational history or telemetry MUST NOT create a persistent relationship linking private participation across protocol layers or action contexts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-007"></a> PE-G4-COMP-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public protocol response, log, status surface, and diagnostic | A public surface MUST NOT expose unnecessary provenance, topology, route, provider, retry, fallback, or execution-path information capable of increasing actor correlation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-008"></a> PE-G4-COMP-008 | MUST | applicable | Every information crossing between Privacy Engine layers | A layer boundary MUST pass only the minimum preserved output required by the receiving layer without carrying unrelated provenance or identity context from the preceding layer. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-009"></a> PE-G4-COMP-009 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation composition exposing multiple individually non-identifying surfaces | The combined surface MUST be assessed and preserved as non-identifying rather than relying only on the independent safety of each component. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-comp-010"></a> PE-G4-COMP-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every informative example, supporting cross-reference, test artifact, assessment record, and documentation surface | Supporting or informative material MUST NOT introduce a correlation or identity interpretation prohibited from normative runtime surfaces. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseobs-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEOBS-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Public comparison output MUST be limited to U's action-bounded identity and exactly one bounded result or failure classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseobs-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEOBS-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A comparison result MUST NOT become an independent first-class protocol subject or reusable public participant identifier. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseobs-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEOBS-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Diagnostics, logs, events, databases and application observations MUST remain non-authoritative for comparison, recovery and commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S47, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseobs-004"></a> PE-G4-TSEOBS-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | An observability surface MUST NOT disclose a blind, opening, confidential canonical component or actor-bearing correlation value. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseobs-005"></a> PE-G4-TSEOBS-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | Absence or inconsistency of non-authoritative observability MUST NOT alter an authoritative comparison result or occurrence state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S47, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-04 / RF-15 — Anonymous membership, continuity, and enrollment separation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-001"></a> PE-G4-MEM-001 | MUST | applicable | Every anonymous membership identity in the current published protocol generation | Anonymous membership identity MUST derive solely from private `memberSecret` possession through `leaf = Poseidon1(memberSecret)`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-002"></a> PE-G4-MEM-002 | MUST | applicable | Every claim of anonymous membership continuity | Anonymous membership continuity MUST mean possession of the same original `memberSecret`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-003"></a> PE-G4-MEM-003 | MUST | applicable | Every replacement of an active `memberSecret` with a different secret | A different `memberSecret` MUST establish a different anonymous membership identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-004"></a> PE-G4-MEM-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every wallet, backend, application, deployment, transport, and heuristic recovery mechanism | A mechanism other than possession of the same original `memberSecret` MUST NOT establish, reconstruct, or simulate anonymous membership continuity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-005"></a> PE-G4-MEM-005 | MUST | applicable | Every membership leaf | A membership leaf MUST remain bounded to membership inclusion, path acquisition, and private proof-witness use according to the applicable protocol semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-006"></a> PE-G4-MEM-006 | MUST | applicable | Every membership enrollment or activation | Enrollment or activation MUST attach membership only to the privately controlled membership material presented for that enrollment without importing funding provenance into membership identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-007"></a> PE-G4-MEM-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every enrollment right, admission object, funding wallet, sponsor relationship, and accounting state | An enrollment or admission artifact MUST NOT be represented as anonymous membership identity or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-mem-008"></a> PE-G4-MEM-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public enrollment, membership-status, readiness, and application surface | A public surface MUST NOT expose a reusable mapping from enrollment or admission provenance to anonymous membership or public action occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | STATE |

## SF-04 / RF-19 — Nullifier-based uniqueness and action-bounded unlinkability

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-001"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-001 | MUST | applicable | Every anonymous action type requiring one action per member per action context | Uniqueness MUST be enforced within the declared `(actionId, member)` scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-002"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-002 | MUST | applicable | Every nullifier in the current published protocol generation | A nullifier MUST be derived as `Poseidon3(memberSecret, actionId, SIGN_DOMAIN)`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-003"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-003 | MUST | applicable | Every action-layer duplicate-prevention decision in the current published protocol generation | Nullifier status MUST remain the sole action-layer duplicate-prevention primitive. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-004"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public nullifier and nullifier-status record | A nullifier or its status MUST NOT be represented as Private Actor identity or reusable participant identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-005"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every nullifier outside its declared action context | A nullifier MUST NOT be reused as a cross-action join key, participant tag, continuity handle, or global identity mechanism. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-006"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every wallet identifier, membership leaf, enrollment identifier, admission object, and application account | A wallet, leaf, enrollment, admission, or application identifier MUST NOT replace the nullifier as the action-layer uniqueness key. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-007"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every duplicate-status response, log, diagnostic, event, and read model | Duplicate-status observability MUST NOT disclose or enable inference of the Private Actor beyond the bounded action-local uniqueness fact. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-uniq-008"></a> PE-G4-UNIQ-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every interpretation of nullifier-enforced uniqueness | Scoped nullifier uniqueness MUST NOT be represented as one-person, one-human, one-wallet, or globally reusable participant uniqueness. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |

## SF-05 / RF-12 — Interface contracts and concrete interface specifications

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-001"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Interface Contract | An Interface Contract MUST possess one stable identity, one bounded semantic role, and one version sufficient to distinguish its published meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-002"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Interface Contract | An Interface Contract MUST derive from exactly one identified published Privacy Engine protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-003"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Interface Contract | An Interface Contract MUST define its bounded input semantics, output semantics, Protocol Operations, externally relied-upon behavior, and applicable observable failure semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-004"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Interface Contract and every concrete realization of it | Interface meaning and concrete representation MUST remain distinct semantic and syntactic roles. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-005"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Concrete Interface Specification | A Concrete Interface Specification MUST possess one stable specification identity, one specification version, and one bounded integration surface. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-006"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Concrete Interface Specification | A Concrete Interface Specification MUST identify and realize exactly one governing Interface Contract. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-007"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Concrete Interface Specification | A Concrete Interface Specification MUST completely define the operation names, invocation forms, field ordering, type constraints, encoding rules, result representations, and externally observable error representations required by its bounded surface. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-008"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Concrete Interface Specification and its governing Interface Contract | The concrete representation MUST preserve semantic equivalence with the governing Interface Contract. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-009"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-009 | MAY | applicable | Every Interface Contract permitting multiple concrete realizations | Multiple Concrete Interface Specifications MAY realize the same Interface Contract when each realization independently preserves the complete bounded interface meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-010"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-010 | MUST | applicable | Every representation-only interface change | A representation-only change MUST preserve the identity, semantics, ordering requirements, compatibility boundary, and observable guarantees of the governing Interface Contract. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-011"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-011 | MUST | applicable | Every change to published Interface Contract meaning | A change to published interface semantics, obligations, validity, or observable guarantees MUST be represented through the applicable distinct-generation and versioning rules. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-012"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Interface Contract and Concrete Interface Specification | Implementation algorithms, storage layouts, deployment topology, transport machinery, operational policy, and application-defined meaning MUST NOT become interface semantics unless the published protocol generation expressly defines them as such. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-013"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Interface Contract and Concrete Interface Specification | An Interface Contract or Concrete Interface Specification MUST NOT independently acquire, transfer, replace, or modify protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-iface-014"></a> PE-G4-IFACE-014 | MUST | applicable | Every public Interface Contract and Concrete Interface Specification | Every public interface realization MUST preserve the applicable SF-03 and SF-04 identity, privacy, unlinkability, minimum-disclosure, and neutral-naming requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseiface-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEIFACE-001 | MAY | applicable | ALWAYS | A concrete interface MAY represent protocol-owned authorization and result fields only with the exact published syntax and semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseiface-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEIFACE-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | An interface, adapter or implementation MUST NOT define a predicate, infer a missing coordinate, select recovery or substitute an implementation value for authoritative protocol state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseiface-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEIFACE-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A public interface MUST NOT carry a confidential opening unless that interface is the authenticated confidential comparison boundary defined by the profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-051"></a> PE-G4-CAP-051 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | At this proposed adapter boundary each declared value SHALL be one complete canonical TSEV1 value of the exact declared type, with no trailing bytes, normalization of malformed input, reordering, reduction or defaults. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S81#carrier | PE-G3-TSEPROF-003, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-052"></a> PE-G4-CAP-052 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | The 94 ordered field assignments and matching type/owner/anchor/equality declarations SHALL be exactly those in the retained dispatch. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S81#carrier | PE-G3-TSEPROF-003, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-053"></a> PE-G4-CAP-053 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A producer crossing the boundary MUST establish independent competence, exact assertion/evidence binding, required source-defined validity and authentication for each supplied fact before it is consumed as authoritative. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S81#assembly | PE-G3-TSEPROF-003, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004, PE-G3-TSEIFACE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-054"></a> PE-G4-CAP-054 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | ReadObject flags MUST be results of that validation, never unchecked caller declarations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S81#assembly | PE-G3-TSEPROF-003, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004, PE-G3-TSEIFACE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-055"></a> PE-G4-CAP-055 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Parsing or mutually consistent binding tuples MUST NOT substitute for source truth or externally fixed A/E/C. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S81#assembly | PE-G3-TSEPROF-003, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004, PE-G3-TSEIFACE-001 |

## SF-05 / RF-13 — Protocol surfaces, operations, and Published Results

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-001"></a> PE-G4-SURF-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Surface | A Protocol Surface MUST possess one stable identity and one bounded externally relied-upon capability scope within one published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-002"></a> PE-G4-SURF-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Surface | A Protocol Surface MUST expose only the protocol capabilities, semantic guarantees, compatibility boundaries, and observable behavior defined for that surface. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-003"></a> PE-G4-SURF-003 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation realizing a Protocol Surface | Internal implementation machinery MUST remain replaceable when the complete published semantics and guarantees of the Protocol Surface remain preserved. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-004"></a> PE-G4-SURF-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation detail behind a Protocol Surface | Code structure, storage layout, deployment topology, relay selection, transport, persistence, monitoring, optimization, or helper naming MUST NOT become protocol-surface semantics merely because a current implementation uses it. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-005"></a> PE-G4-SURF-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Operation | A Protocol Operation MUST possess one stable capability identity defined by one published Interface Contract within one identified protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-006"></a> PE-G4-SURF-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Operation | A Protocol Operation MUST define one bounded protocol-owned capability, including its applicable input meaning, output meaning, and externally relied-upon behavior. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-007"></a> PE-G4-SURF-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Operation and associated Protocol Action | The exposed Protocol Operation and associated Protocol Action representation MUST preserve the RF-07 distinction between the Protocol Operation capability type and each Protocol Action occurrence classified by that capability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-008"></a> PE-G4-SURF-008 | MUST | applicable | Every offered Protocol Operation | The governing Interface Contract MUST declare the operation's applicability conditions and the semantic roles of every required input and Published Result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-009"></a> PE-G4-SURF-009 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation realizing a Protocol Operation | An implementation MUST preserve the operation's published capability across execution and implementation variation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-018"></a> PE-G4-SURF-018 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every execution or implementation variation realizing a Protocol Operation | Execution or implementation variation MUST NOT redefine the Protocol Operation's published capability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-010"></a> PE-G4-SURF-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Result | A Published Result MUST possess one bounded result identity and MUST identify the governing Interface Contract and Protocol Operation from which its meaning derives. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S31, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-011"></a> PE-G4-SURF-011 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Result | A Published Result MUST express only the interface-level success semantics, failure semantics, guarantees, and observable meaning defined by its governing Interface Contract. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S31, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-012"></a> PE-G4-SURF-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Result and concrete representation of it | Published Result meaning and its serialized, transported, stored, displayed, or application-facing representation MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-013"></a> PE-G4-SURF-013 | MUST | applicable | Every concrete realization or translation of a Published Result | Every concrete realization or permitted downstream translation MUST preserve the complete bounded meaning of the same Published Result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-014"></a> PE-G4-SURF-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Published Result | A Published Result MUST NOT be represented as a Protocol Action Evaluation, Protocol Action Determination, acceptance object, rejection object, or separate Evaluation Outcome object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-015"></a> PE-G4-SURF-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Published Result | A Published Result MUST NOT be represented as a committed Protocol State Transition or as an independently authoritative public protocol-state consequence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-016"></a> PE-G4-SURF-016 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application interpretation or representation of a Protocol Surface, Protocol Operation, or Published Result | Application-defined meaning, terminology, formatting, workflow, or response structure MUST NOT redefine protocol capability, validity, or Published Result meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S09, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-017"></a> PE-G4-SURF-017 | MUST | applicable | Every public Protocol Surface, Protocol Operation representation, and Published Result | The complete exposed composition MUST preserve the applicable SF-03 and SF-04 identity, privacy, unlinkability, minimum-disclosure, and forbidden-correlation requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-surf-019"></a> PE-G4-SURF-019 | MUST | applicable | Every Determination and every later representation or realization of it | Protocol-semantic Determination production MUST remain distinct from representation, serialization, persistence, publication, transport, operational delivery, display, indexing, events, logs, responses and application interpretation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseresult-001"></a> PE-G4-TSERESULT-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every completed comparison attempt MUST produce exactly one of equivalent, material-conflict or indeterminate. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseresult-002"></a> PE-G4-TSERESULT-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A represented comparison result MUST preserve its exact authoritative classification and U binding without expanding its evidentiary claim. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseresult-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSERESULT-003-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A commitment digest MUST establish only computational binding of its encoded preimage under the identified algorithm. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseresult-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSERESULT-003-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A commitment digest MUST NOT prove any unencoded semantic or identity claim. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |

## SF-06 / RF-16 — Proofs and declared public inputs

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-001"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-001 | MUST | applicable | Every proof required by a Privacy Engine Protocol Action | A proof MUST possess one bounded evidence identity distinct from its encoding, public inputs, private witness, supported Protocol Action, and Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-002"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-002 | MUST | applicable | Every proof type in a published protocol generation | The published protocol generation MUST declare the proof relation, proof-system requirements, public-input schema, private-witness boundary, and verification semantics applicable to the proof type. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-003"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-003 | MUST | applicable | Every current-generation anonymous-action proof | The proof relation MUST establish knowledge of the applicable membership secret and membership witness, inclusion under the declared membership root, and correct nullifier derivation for the declared action context without disclosing the Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-004"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-004 | MUST | applicable | Every proof relation and proof-construction boundary | Public inputs and private witness material MUST remain explicitly separated according to the applicable published proof relation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-005"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-005 | MUST | applicable | Every current-generation anonymous-action public-input set | The declared public inputs MUST occur in the exact ordered sequence `nullifier`, `petitionId` or `actionId`, `membershipRoot`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-006"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-006 | MUST | applicable | Every current-generation declared public-input interpretation | `nullifier` MUST remain bounded to action-context uniqueness, `petitionId` or `actionId` MUST remain bounded to action context, and `membershipRoot` MUST remain bounded to the applicable membership-state commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-007"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-007 | MUST | applicable | Every proof and declared public-input set presented together | The proof representation, public-input values, ordering, action context, and proof relation MUST form one coherent verification input. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-008"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-008 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-construction process | Proof construction MUST keep `memberSecret`, Merkle path, membership leaf, and other private witness material outside public inputs and public protocol surfaces. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-009"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-009 | MUST | applicable | Every proof realization for the current published generation | The proof realization MUST use the published generation's declared Circom and Groth16-over-BN254 proof semantics and compatible verifier artifacts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-010"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-010 | MUST | applicable | Every published proof artifact set | Proof artifacts, verification keys, verifier artifacts, public-input ordering, and associated provenance MUST satisfy the published artifact-integrity and reproducibility requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-011"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-011 | MUST | applicable | Every permitted proof or public-input representation | A representation MUST preserve the complete proof relation, proof-system semantics, verifier compatibility, input meaning, and input ordering of the identified published generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-012"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-012 | MUST | applicable | Every change to a proof system, proof relation, public-input schema, public-input ordering, verifier encoding, or artifact-integrity rule | The change MUST follow the applicable distinct-generation, versioning, publication, and artifact-regeneration requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-013"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof and proof representation | A proof or its representation MUST NOT be treated as the Protocol Action occurrence, Private Actor identity, wallet identity, membership identity, application truth, or reusable participant identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-proof-014"></a> PE-G4-PROOF-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every declared public input and public-input composition | A public input or public-input composition MUST NOT exceed its declared role or become a reusable participant identifier, cross-action join key, actor-reconstruction value, or application-only correlation field. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |

## SF-06 / RF-17 — Proof verification

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-001"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-001 | MUST | applicable | Every required proof verification | Proof verification MUST occur as one bounded verification occurrence whose proof, public inputs, proof relation, verifier artifacts, and result are distinguishable. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
