| <a id="pe-g4-verify-002"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-002 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-verification occurrence | Verification MUST evaluate exactly the presented proof and declared public-input set against the applicable published proof relation and verifier artifacts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-003"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-003 | MUST | applicable | Every correct proof-verification realization | Verification MUST be deterministic for the same proof, public inputs, proof relation, verifier artifacts, and protocol-relevant verification context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-004"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-004 | MUST | applicable | Every completed proof-verification occurrence | A completed verification MUST produce exactly one bounded proof-valid or proof-invalid classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-005"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-005 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-valid classification | A proof-valid classification MUST establish only that the proof and declared public inputs satisfy the applicable published proof relation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-006"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-006 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-invalid classification required for a Protocol Action | A required proof-invalid classification MUST prevent that proof from satisfying the corresponding applicable proof-verification requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-007"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-007 | MUST | applicable | Every inability to complete required proof verification | Verification failure MUST remain distinguishable from a completed proof-valid classification, completed proof-invalid classification, Protocol Action rejection, and later failure classifications. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-008"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-008 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-valid verification result used in Protocol Action Evaluation | The result MUST contribute only the bounded evidence established by the applicable proof relation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-009"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-valid verification result | Successful proof verification MUST NOT independently establish complete Protocol Action validity, acceptance, rejection, transition authorization, transition execution, or commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-010"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-verification occurrence and result | Proof verification MUST NOT establish Private Actor identity, wallet identity, enrollment provenance, admission provenance, application truth, legal truth, factual truth, or reusable participant identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-011"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-verification implementation | Verifier code structure, deployment address, transaction identity, runtime mechanism, storage, transport, or diagnostic behavior MUST NOT redefine the applicable proof relation or verification semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-verify-012"></a> PE-G4-VERIFY-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-verification component and result | A proof-verification component or result MUST NOT independently acquire or modify protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-034"></a> PE-G4-CAP-034 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A consumed verification result MUST bind the bounded V, exact Z/proof representation, ordered PI=(n,a,r), relation, compatible exact verifier/artifact material and protocol-relevant context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S79#qualification | PE-G3-VERIFY-001, PE-G3-VERIFY-002, PE-G3-PROOF-007, PE-G3-PROOF-010 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-035"></a> PE-G4-CAP-035 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | The competent source MUST establish that every computation required by those identified semantics completed before classifying proof-valid or proof-invalid. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S79#qualification | PE-G3-VERIFY-001, PE-G3-VERIFY-002, PE-G3-PROOF-007, PE-G3-PROOF-010 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-036"></a> PE-G4-CAP-036 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A raw FALSE MUST be usable as a completed proof-invalid result only when the governing source establishes that it denotes completed negative verification for the exact inputs. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S79#evidence | PE-G3-VERIFY-004, PE-G3-VERIFY-006, PE-G3-VERIFY-007 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-037"></a> PE-G4-CAP-037 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | If the same return can denote failure to complete required computations, a consumer MUST require competent independent completion/binding evidence or a faithful realization that preserves the distinction. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S79#evidence | PE-G3-VERIFY-004, PE-G3-VERIFY-006, PE-G3-VERIFY-007 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-038"></a> PE-G4-CAP-038 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | An unchecked completion flag, revert label or unappointed trace MUST NOT discharge this duty. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S79#evidence | PE-G3-VERIFY-004, PE-G3-VERIFY-006, PE-G3-VERIFY-007 |

## SF-06 / RF-18 — Evidence, protocol validity, and non-proof validity conditions

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-001"></a> PE-G4-VALID-001 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-verification result used during Protocol Action Evaluation | The result MUST remain bounded evidence concerning exactly the proof relation and public-input relationship evaluated by that verification occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-002"></a> PE-G4-VALID-002 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-verification result contributing to complete Evaluation | The result MUST contribute to the later Protocol Action Evaluation without replacing the Action, applicable rule set, Evaluation, or Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-003"></a> PE-G4-VALID-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action validity determination basis | Complete Protocol Action validity MUST mean satisfaction of every applicable protocol rule within one bounded evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-004"></a> PE-G4-VALID-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every successful proof-verification result | Successful proof verification MUST NOT be represented as complete Protocol Action validity unless proof verification constitutes the complete applicable protocol-rule set for that action and context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-005"></a> PE-G4-VALID-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action whose applicable rule set includes non-proof validity conditions | Every applicable non-proof validity condition MUST remain represented and evaluated as part of the complete applicable protocol-rule set. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-006"></a> PE-G4-VALID-006 | MUST | applicable | Every non-proof protocol-validity condition | A non-proof validity condition MUST derive from the identified published protocol generation and MUST possess a bounded applicability rule for the affected action and context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-007"></a> PE-G4-VALID-007 | MUST | applicable | Every current-generation Protocol Action Evaluation basis | The applicable validity basis MUST account for required action shape, public-input coherence, accepted-root status, nullifier uniqueness, replay prevention, domain separation, protocol-state requirements, and every other applicable generation-derived condition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-008"></a> PE-G4-VALID-008 | MUST | applicable | Every required proof-invalid verification result | A required proof-invalid result MUST prevent the Protocol Action from satisfying the corresponding applicable proof requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-009"></a> PE-G4-VALID-009 | MUST | applicable | Every interpretation of protocol validity | Protocol validity MUST remain action-specific, applicable-rule-relative, and bounded to the identified evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-010"></a> PE-G4-VALID-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-valid or protocol-valid classification | Proof validity or protocol validity MUST NOT independently become Protocol Action acceptance, rejection, transition authorization, transition execution, or commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-011"></a> PE-G4-VALID-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every protocol-valid Protocol Action and interpretation of it | Protocol validity MUST NOT be represented as application truth, external factual truth, legal truth, social truth, institutional recognition, or a Protocol Claim. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-valid-012"></a> PE-G4-VALID-012 | MUST | applicable | Every evidence, validity, failure, and diagnostic surface | The surface MUST preserve SF-03 and SF-04 identity separation, Private Actor non-disclosure, role-bounded public values, neutral naming, and compositional unlinkability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-001"></a> PE-G4-CAP-001 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | For the selected capability, H MUST be the finite authoritative sequence of valid membership-root introductions in its governing membership scope at the applicable cut. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-domain | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-002"></a> PE-G4-CAP-002 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Root eligibility MUST use exactly its latest min(16,&#124;H&#124;) introduction occurrences, newest first. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-domain | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-003"></a> PE-G4-CAP-003 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A distinct valid introduction of an equal value MUST count separately; a retransmission or repeated observation of one introduction MUST NOT count again. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-domain | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-004"></a> PE-G4-CAP-004 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | An initial root MUST count only if authoritatively introduced. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-domain | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-005"></a> PE-G4-CAP-005 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | The declared root r MUST be eligible exactly when it equals the root of at least one occupied introduction in that suffix. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-acceptance | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-006"></a> PE-G4-CAP-006 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | An empty history MUST yield ineligibility. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-acceptance | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-007"></a> PE-G4-CAP-007 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | An unoccupied slot MUST NOT confer eligibility, including for zero; an occupied zero MUST receive no privilege or exclusion beyond the governing membership-state validity rules. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#root-acceptance | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-VALID-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-056"></a> PE-G4-CAP-056 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Root production MUST establish valid introduction identities, authoritative order/count, complete newest suffix, scalar domain, occupancy and exact scope/policy/cut/r binding. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#root | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-057"></a> PE-G4-CAP-057 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Occupied positions MUST be h,h-1,...,h-min(16,h)+1 with source-owned nonempty occurrence identities; absent positions/values MUST be zero and absent identity bytes empty. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#root | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-058"></a> PE-G4-CAP-058 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Shape equality MUST NOT establish validity, authority or currentness. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#root | PE-G3-PROOF-006, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |

## SF-07 / RF-20 — Protocol Actions

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-action-001"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Protocol Action | A Protocol Action MUST be one bounded occurrence of protocol-defined behavior presented through an implementation for Evaluation under the applicable rules of one identified published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-002"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action occurrence | Each Protocol Action MUST possess one stable occurrence identity distinct from every other Protocol Action occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-003"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action | The Action MUST be bounded to one occurrence, one action type, one declared public-input set, one applicable protocol-rule set, and one bounded evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-004"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action occurrence | The occurrence MUST identify its protocol-defined action type and governing published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-005"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Operation, action type, and Protocol Action occurrence | Published capability, action-type classification, and individual action occurrence MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-006"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-006 | MUST | applicable | Every effort to construct, present, submit, or obtain evaluation of a candidate Protocol Action | An action attempt MUST remain distinct from a Protocol Action occurrence admitted to the authoritative evaluation boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-007"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-007 | MUST | applicable | Every candidate admitted to authoritative Protocol Action Evaluation | Admission to the applicable authoritative evaluation boundary MUST instantiate one bounded Protocol Action occurrence without identifying its Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-008"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transport retry, relay attempt, submission retry, implementation retry, and internal processing attempt | An operational retry or attempt MUST NOT silently widen, replace, merge, or create the identity of the logical Protocol Action occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-009"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-009 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol rule governing repeated authoritative presentations | The rule MUST determine whether the presentations concern one action occurrence or distinct action occurrences without inferring or merging Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-010"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action | The Action MUST carry exactly the declared public-input set required for its action type while remaining distinct from those public inputs. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-011"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-011 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-supported Protocol Action | The Action and its supporting proof MUST remain distinct, and the proof MUST contribute only its bounded evidence role. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-012"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action performed through an implementation | The Action occurrence MUST remain distinct from the implementation, deployment, transaction, transport, storage, and runtime machinery through which it is presented. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-013"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-013 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Private Actor and Protocol Action | Protocol Action representation, processing, and Evaluation presentation MUST preserve the RF-09 Private Actor and public Protocol Action separation invariant throughout the Action lifecycle. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S19, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-014"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Action identifier and representation | An action identifier or representation MUST NOT identify, encode, imply, reconstruct, or become a reusable identifier for the Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-015"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application interpretation of a Protocol Action | Application-defined meaning, terminology, workflow, metadata, or truth MUST NOT become Protocol Action identity or protocol validity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-016"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-016 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship from a Protocol Action to a possible Published Result, Determination, or Protocol State Transition | The Protocol Action MUST remain distinct from every interface result, downstream Determination, and possible state-transition consequence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-action-017"></a> PE-G4-ACTION-017 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Action interpretation and extension | A Protocol Action MUST NOT be represented as a Protocol Claim or used to introduce another first-class subject without satisfying the established closed-ontology test. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-021"></a> PE-G4-CAP-021 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Before authoritative admission for this capability, n,a,r MUST each be available in their declared canonical representation and denote a scalar x with 0 <= x < 21888242871839275222246405745257275088548364400416034343698204186575808495617. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#scalar | PE-G3-PROOF-005, PE-G3-PROOF-009, PE-G3-FAIL-018 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-022"></a> PE-G4-CAP-022 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Malformed representation, completed out-of-domain assessment, or inability to establish that assessment MUST admit no A, begin no E, produce no D, acceptance or rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#scalar | PE-G3-PROOF-005, PE-G3-PROOF-009, PE-G3-FAIL-018 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-023"></a> PE-G4-CAP-023 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Modular reduction, truncation or a default value MUST NOT substitute for that assessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#scalar | PE-G3-PROOF-005, PE-G3-PROOF-009, PE-G3-FAIL-018 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-027"></a> PE-G4-CAP-027 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Each new authoritative admission for evaluation MUST instantiate a distinct bounded A even when proof bytes or PI equal an earlier admission. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#occurrence | PE-G3-ACTION-002, PE-G3-ACTION-008, PE-G3-ACTION-009 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-028"></a> PE-G4-CAP-028 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Transport, retransmission, delivery or retrieval alone MUST NOT establish a new authoritative admission or create A/E/D. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#occurrence | PE-G3-ACTION-002, PE-G3-ACTION-008, PE-G3-ACTION-009 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-029"></a> PE-G4-CAP-029 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Admission identity MUST NOT be inferred from proof equality, nullifier equality, actor identity or endpoint arrival. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#occurrence | PE-G3-ACTION-002, PE-G3-ACTION-008, PE-G3-ACTION-009 |

## SF-07 / RF-21 — Applicable protocol-rule sets and action validity

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-001"></a> PE-G4-RULE-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action | The Action MUST be governed by exactly one complete and bounded applicable protocol-rule set for its identified action type and evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-002"></a> PE-G4-RULE-002 | MUST | applicable | Every rule included in an applicable Protocol Action rule set | Each applicable rule MUST derive from the identified published protocol generation through the established Protocol Specification and implementation-profile direction. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-003"></a> PE-G4-RULE-003 | MUST | applicable | Every determination of Protocol Action rule applicability | Applicability MUST be determined only from protocol-defined action type, applicable implementation profile, protocol-relevant state, and bounded evaluation-context conditions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-004"></a> PE-G4-RULE-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action admitted for authoritative Evaluation | The complete applicable protocol-rule set MUST be established before authoritative Evaluation begins. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-005"></a> PE-G4-RULE-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation, application, transport, deployment, and operational policy | Implementation convenience, application policy, transport behavior, deployment state, or operational discretion MUST NOT silently create, remove, weaken, strengthen, or reinterpret a protocol-validity rule. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-006"></a> PE-G4-RULE-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action validity classification basis | The validity-classification basis MUST apply the complete RF-18 definition of Protocol Action validity through the complete applicable protocol-rule set within the identified bounded evaluation context without replacing or redefining that definition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-007"></a> PE-G4-RULE-007 | MUST | applicable | Every applicable Protocol Action rule set | The rule set MUST include every applicable proof requirement, public-input requirement, non-proof validity condition, uniqueness condition, state-relative condition, and action-type constraint. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-008"></a> PE-G4-RULE-008 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol-validity interpretation | Protocol-rule interpretation and application MUST preserve the RF-18 action-specific, applicable-rule-relative, evaluation-context-bounded validity invariant without widening validity beyond the identified context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-013"></a> PE-G4-RULE-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every comparison of Protocol Action validity across materially different evaluation contexts | Validity in one evaluation context MUST NOT be inferred as validity in a materially different evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-009"></a> PE-G4-RULE-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application interpretation and external claim associated with a Protocol Action | Application meaning, factual meaning, legal meaning, social meaning, or institutional meaning MUST NOT replace or redefine the applicable protocol-rule set or protocol validity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S28, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-010"></a> PE-G4-RULE-010 | MUST | applicable | Every action-local uniqueness or authorization rule | The rule MUST preserve the declared action scope without creating reusable participant identity, actor disclosure, or unauthorized cross-action linkage. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-011"></a> PE-G4-RULE-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation, transaction, transport, retry, storage, and diagnostic identifier | An operational identifier MUST NOT determine, replace, merge, or redefine Protocol Action identity, rule applicability, or protocol validity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-rule-012"></a> PE-G4-RULE-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action prepared for complete Evaluation | The Action, complete applicable rule set, and bounded evaluation context MUST be available as distinct preserved inputs to SF-08 without pre-emptively defining Evaluation, Determination, acceptance, or rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |

## SF-08 / RF-22 — Protocol Action Evaluation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-001"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation | A Protocol Action Evaluation MUST possess one stable occurrence identity identifying exactly one bounded Evaluation occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-002"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation | An Evaluation MUST evaluate exactly one identified Protocol Action. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-003"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation | An Evaluation MUST apply exactly one complete applicable protocol-rule set established for the evaluated Action. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-004"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation | An Evaluation MUST occur within exactly one bounded protocol-relevant evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-005"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-005 | MUST | applicable | Every candidate entering the authoritative Evaluation boundary | Authoritative Evaluation MUST begin only after the Action, complete applicable rule set, and bounded evaluation context have been established as distinct inputs. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-006"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation after Evaluation begins | The Evaluation MUST preserve its identified Action, complete applicable rule set, and bounded evaluation context throughout the Evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-007"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation after Evaluation begins | An Evaluation MUST NOT substitute, merge, widen, narrow, repair, or infer a replacement Action, rule set, or evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-008"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-008 | MUST | applicable | Every complete Protocol Action Evaluation | A complete Evaluation MUST evaluate every applicable protocol rule governing the identified Action in the identified context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-009"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-009 | MUST | applicable | Every Evaluation component | An Evaluation component MUST possess an explicit assignment to one applicable requirement or one bounded subset of the complete applicable rule set. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-010"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Evaluation component | An Evaluation component MUST evaluate only the applicable requirement or bounded subset assigned to that component. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-011"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Evaluation component whose assigned rules are not the complete applicable rule set | A bounded component MUST NOT be represented as complete Protocol Action Evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-012"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Evaluation containing multiple required components | The Evaluation MUST coherently combine every required component result before determining whether the Evaluation is complete. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-013"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-013 | MUST | applicable | Every proof-verification occurrence contributing to Evaluation | Proof verification MUST remain bounded evidence concerning the declared proof relation and declared public inputs. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-014"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-014 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between proof verification and Protocol Action Evaluation | Proof verification MUST remain distinct from the complete Evaluation to which it contributes. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-015"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-verification result | A proof-verification result MUST NOT directly produce a Protocol Action Determination, acceptance classification, or rejection classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-016"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-016 | MUST | applicable | Every Evaluation whose applicable rule set contains non-proof validity conditions | The Evaluation MUST evaluate every applicable non-proof validity condition independently of proof-verification success. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-017"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-017 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation completeness classification | An Evaluation MUST be classified as complete exactly when it has evaluated every applicable rule for the identified Action within the identified context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-018"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-018 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every partial or incomplete Protocol Action Evaluation | A partial or incomplete Evaluation MUST NOT produce a Determination, acceptance classification, or rejection classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-019"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-019 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Evaluation failure and every pre-evaluation failure | Evaluation failure or pre-evaluation failure MUST NOT be represented as a completed Evaluation or as producing a Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-020"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-020 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Evaluation component, identifier, evidence record, representation, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, and observability surface | An Evaluation surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, extend evidence into actor identification, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-021"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-021 | MUST | applicable | Every completed Protocol Action Evaluation | Every completed Protocol Action Evaluation MUST produce exactly one Protocol Action Determination as part of the same protocol-semantic completion transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-022"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-022 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation representation of Evaluation completion and Determination production | An implementation MUST NOT represent Evaluation completion and required Determination production as two protocol-semantic transitions separated by a valid completed-Evaluation state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-023"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-023 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation completion classification | A Protocol Action Evaluation MUST be in a valid completed state exactly when every applicable completion requirement is satisfied and exactly one corresponding Protocol Action Determination has been produced. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-024"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-024 | MAY | applicable | Every active incomplete Protocol Action Evaluation | An active Protocol Action Evaluation MAY contain bounded internal steps, completed components and intermediate component state while remaining incomplete. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-025"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-025 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every internal Evaluation step, component result, intermediate state and readiness condition | An internal step, component result, combined intermediate state or completion-readiness condition MUST NOT be represented as completed Evaluation or as a Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-eval-026"></a> PE-G4-EVAL-026 | MUST | applicable | Every begun Evaluation unable to establish one valid Determination at its completion boundary | If an Evaluation that has begun cannot establish exactly one valid Determination at the same protocol-semantic boundary as Evaluation completion, the Evaluation MUST terminate as Evaluation failure, MUST remain incomplete, and MUST produce no Determination, acceptance or rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsereeval-001-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEREEVAL-001-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A new Evaluation MUST create a new E identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsereeval-001-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEREEVAL-001-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A new Evaluation MUST preserve every earlier E and D without mutation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsereeval-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEREEVAL-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A new Evaluation MUST produce a new D only if it completes successfully at the Evaluation-completion boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsereeval-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEREEVAL-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Failure before successful completion of a new Evaluation MUST produce no new D, acceptance, authorization or U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-024"></a> PE-G4-CAP-024 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | After authoritative admission, the identified inputs MUST remain fixed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S78#admission | PE-G3-EVAL-001, PE-G3-EVAL-005, PE-G3-PROOF-007 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-025"></a> PE-G4-CAP-025 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Before E begins, A, its type, complete R and bounded coherent C MUST be established as distinct inputs; if R or C cannot be established, E MUST NOT begin. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S78#admission | PE-G3-EVAL-001, PE-G3-EVAL-005, PE-G3-PROOF-007 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-026"></a> PE-G4-CAP-026 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Missing later facts MUST NOT permit substitution of a new r, source scope, context or rule set within that E. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S78#admission | PE-G3-EVAL-001, PE-G3-EVAL-005, PE-G3-PROOF-007 |

## SF-08 / RF-23 — Protocol Action Determination

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-001"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination | A Protocol Action Determination MUST possess one stable result identity identifying exactly one bounded Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-002"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-002 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Protocol Action Evaluation and its Determination | The Evaluation process and resulting Determination MUST remain separately identified and semantically distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-003"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-003 | MUST | applicable | Every completed Protocol Action Evaluation | A completed Evaluation MUST produce exactly one Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-004"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination | A Determination MUST derive from exactly one completed Protocol Action Evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-005"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination | A Determination MUST identify exactly one producing completed Evaluation, one evaluated Action, one complete applicable rule set, and one bounded evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-006"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-006 | MUST | applicable | Every valid Protocol Action Determination | A valid Determination MUST contain and reference its RF-24 classification without defining an alternative classification model. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-007"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete or failed Protocol Action Evaluation | An incomplete or failed Evaluation MUST NOT be treated as possessing or producing a Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-008"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination and proof-verification result | A Determination MUST remain distinct from every proof-verification occurrence and proof-verification result contributing to its Evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-009"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-009 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination and Published Result | A Determination MUST remain distinct from every interface-level Published Result and its concrete representation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-010"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination and possible Protocol State Transition | A Determination MUST remain distinct from transition identity, transition execution, commitment, and every committed public protocol-state consequence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-011"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Determination identifier, representation, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, and observability surface | A Determination surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, become reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-012"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-012 | MUST | applicable | Every pair of correct Evaluations of the same Action under the same applicable rules and equivalent contexts | The Evaluations MUST produce equivalent acceptance or rejection Determinations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-013"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-013 | MUST | applicable | Every context-dependent Protocol Action Determination | The Determination MUST remain bounded to its identified evaluation context without converting material context differences into implementation discretion. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-014"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Action Determination | A Determination MUST NOT be represented as an Evaluation Outcome object or as an independently authoritative source of protocol semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-015"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every protocol state representing Evaluation completion | A protocol state MUST NOT contain a completed Protocol Action Evaluation without exactly one corresponding Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-determ-016"></a> PE-G4-DETERM-016 | MUST | applicable | Every Evaluation and Determination identity boundary | Atomic establishment of Evaluation completion and Determination production MUST preserve the distinct identities and meanings of the Evaluation process and Determination result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |

## SF-08 / RF-24 — Acceptance and rejection

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-class-001"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-001 | MUST | applicable | Every valid Protocol Action Determination | A valid Determination MUST record exactly one mutually exclusive acceptance or rejection classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-002"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-002 | MUST | applicable | Every correct accepting Determination | Acceptance MUST mean that completed Evaluation established satisfaction of every applicable protocol rule within the identified evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-003"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-003 | MUST | applicable | Every correct rejecting Determination | Rejection MUST mean that completed Evaluation established non-satisfaction of at least one applicable protocol rule within the identified evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-004"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-004 | MUST | applicable | Every correct Protocol Action Determination | The Determination MUST accept the Action exactly when the Action is protocol-valid within the identified evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-005"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-005 | MUST | applicable | Every correct Protocol Action Determination | The Determination MUST reject the Action exactly when the Action is not protocol-valid within the identified evaluation context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-006"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete Evaluation, Evaluation failure, and pre-evaluation failure | An incomplete Evaluation, Evaluation failure, or pre-evaluation failure MUST NOT be classified as acceptance or rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-007"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every infrastructure, transport, deployment, storage, implementation, and observability failure | An operational or implementation failure MUST NOT be represented as protocol rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-008"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every proof-invalid result considered outside complete Protocol Action Evaluation | A proof-invalid result alone MUST NOT be represented as a complete rejecting Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-009"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every erroneous acceptance or rejection classification | An incorrect classification MUST NOT redefine the applicable rules, protocol validity, or protocol semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-010"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every acceptance and rejection classification | Acceptance or rejection MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified acceptance object, rejection object, or Evaluation Outcome object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-011"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application or external interpretation of acceptance or rejection | Acceptance or rejection MUST NOT be treated as establishing application, factual, legal, social, or institutional truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-class-012"></a> PE-G4-CLASS-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every acceptance or rejection representation, identifier, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, and observability surface | An acceptance or rejection classification MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | STATE |

## SF-08 / RF-25 — Accepted no-transition behavior

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-001"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-001 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted Protocol Action Determination | The applicable protocol rules MUST determine whether the accepted Determination requires no authorized Protocol State Transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-002"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-002 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted Determination for which no transition is required | The Determination MUST terminate successfully without an authorized Protocol State Transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-003"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-003 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted no-transition path | The path MUST be treated as a complete successful terminal path at the Determination boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-004"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every accepted no-transition path | An accepted no-transition path MUST NOT be represented as rejection, incomplete Evaluation, Evaluation failure, pre-transition failure, transition failure, incomplete execution, missing commitment, or implementation error. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-005"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every inference from Protocol Action acceptance | Acceptance alone MUST NOT be treated as establishing that a Protocol State Transition exists. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-006"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every inference from Protocol Action acceptance | Acceptance alone MUST NOT be treated as transition authorization. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-007"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every inference from Protocol Action acceptance | Acceptance alone MUST NOT be treated as transition execution or commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-008"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every rejecting Protocol Action Determination | A rejecting Determination MUST NOT authorize a transition that requires an accepted Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-notrans-009"></a> PE-G4-NOTRANS-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation handling an accepted no-transition path | An implementation MUST NOT invent a transition, report missing commitment, or reinterpret the protocol-defined absence of a transition as failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |

## SF-09 / RF-26 — Published transition mappings, preconditions, and eligibility

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-001"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-001 | MUST | applicable | Every published Protocol Transition mapping | A Protocol Transition mapping MUST possess one stable mapping identity within the applicable published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-002"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-002 | MUST | applicable | Every published Protocol Transition mapping | The mapping MUST define the permitted semantic relation between its applicable pre-transition-state class and its protocol-defined successor-state class. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-003"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Transition mapping and every interpretation of it | A Protocol Transition mapping MUST NOT be represented as one Protocol State Transition occurrence, execution, commitment, history entry, or actor occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-004"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-004 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation applying a Protocol Transition mapping | The implementation MUST preserve the mapping identity and published transition semantics unchanged. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-005"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-005 | MUST | applicable | Every published Protocol Transition mapping with transition-specific preconditions | The mapping MUST identify the complete published precondition set governing its eligibility. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-006"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-006 | MUST | applicable | Every transition-eligibility evaluation | Eligibility MUST be established against the applicable protocol state, identified mapping, applicable Action context, and complete published transition-precondition set. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-007"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-007 | MUST | applicable | Every transition-eligibility evaluation whose complete applicable precondition set is satisfied | The eligibility result MUST preserve the identified mapping and permit only the later processing authorized by the applicable protocol rules. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-008"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-008 | MUST | applicable | Every transition-eligibility evaluation whose applicable precondition set is not completely satisfied | The ineligible result MUST preserve the applicable pre-transition protocol state as unchanged by that attempted transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-009"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation evaluating transition preconditions | The implementation MUST NOT infer satisfaction, weaken or strengthen a precondition, substitute another condition, repair the applicable state, or derive an alternate mapping. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-010"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-010 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between transition eligibility and complete Protocol Action Evaluation | Transition eligibility MUST remain a bounded transition-specific evaluation distinct from the complete Evaluation defined by RF-22. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-011"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition-enforcement result | A transition-enforcement result MUST NOT itself establish acceptance or rejection, create or alter a Determination, authorize or execute a transition occurrence, commit state change, or establish successor state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-012"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation-local restriction on operation exposure, request handling, or execution capability | An implementation-local restriction MUST NOT be represented as a published transition precondition or as changing protocol eligibility semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transmap-013"></a> PE-G4-TRANSMAP-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every mapping, precondition, eligibility, enforcement, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, and observability surface | A transition-mapping or eligibility surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-001"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | The successor MUST contain exactly one closed transition-mapping registry with the identity and serialization defined by S69. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-002"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Each registry row MUST bind one mapping identity, path and digest to one profile identity, path and digest. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-003-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Mapping-to-profile cardinality MUST be exactly 1:1. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-003-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Mapping and profile ordinals MUST match. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-004"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | The registry MUST reject duplicates, missing members, digest mismatch, conflicting bindings and unresolved applicability or recovery definitions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-005-a01"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-005-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An empty validated registry MUST authorize zero transitions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-005-a02"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-005-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | An empty validated registry MUST NOT imply missing or inferred mappings. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-006"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A mapping MUST NOT be inferred or discovered from an implementation, interface, runtime, storage, application or observed behaviour. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-007"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-007 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Registry membership, mappings and profiles MUST be immutable within one generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tmreg-008"></a> PE-G4-TMREG-008 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Any registry, mapping or profile change MUST require a distinct future Protocol Generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every mapping MUST reference one separate immutable normative equivalence profile and its SHA-256. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every profile MUST include all scalar, definition, projection and recovery fields required by S70 without blanks or implementation defaults. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every profile definition MUST resolve to one authoritative successor source anchor and one total deterministic PE-TSE-EXPR-V2 expression. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-004"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every component MUST define its type, representation, canonicalization, normalization, authority, availability, validity, authentication, privacy and observability rules. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-005"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-005 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every profile MUST define mutually exclusive equivalence and material-conflict predicates that cover all complete valid inputs. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-006-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-006-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every profile MUST define which differences are immaterial. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-006-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-006-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every profile MUST prove that each immaterial difference is outside the mapping's materiality boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-007"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-007 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every profile MUST define and validate Dep(D) membership and its complete recovery table. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-008-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-008-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An incomplete, ambiguous, cyclic, non-total or privacy-invalid profile MUST be inadmissible. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseprof-008-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEPROF-008-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An incomplete, ambiguous, cyclic, non-total or privacy-invalid profile MUST authorize no transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-062"></a> PE-G4-CAP-062 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Within the validated selected-effect domain, equivalence MUST be v AND NOT b, with ordered sufficient conflicts NOT v then b. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#gate-materiality | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A01, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A02 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-063"></a> PE-G4-CAP-063 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Authorized v/b MUST be TRUE/FALSE. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#gate-materiality | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A01, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A02 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-064"></a> PE-G4-CAP-064 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Every other source-required material condition MUST be explicitly integrated before admission. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#gate-materiality | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A01, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A02 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-065"></a> PE-G4-CAP-065 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | All original comparison result/failure rows, including both-true and incomplete coverage, MUST remain. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#gate-materiality | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A01, PE-G3-TSEPROF-006-A02 |

## SF-09 / RF-27 — Protocol State Transition occurrence identity and lifecycle

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-001"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition | A Protocol State Transition MUST possess one stable occurrence identity identifying exactly one bounded protocol-governed state-change occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-002"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition | The occurrence MUST retain one bounded lifecycle across every applicable authorization, execution, commitment, and failure stage. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-003"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-003 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Protocol Action and a Protocol State Transition | The Action and transition occurrence MUST retain distinct identities and semantic roles. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-004"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-004 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Protocol Action Evaluation and a Protocol State Transition | The Evaluation and transition occurrence MUST retain distinct identities and semantic roles. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-005"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-005 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Protocol Action Determination and a Protocol State Transition | The Determination and transition occurrence MUST retain distinct identities and semantic roles. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-006"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition occurrence | The occurrence MUST remain distinct from its governing mapping, pre-transition state, successor state, implementation, history representation, and public consequence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-007"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-007 | MUST | applicable | Every authorization, execution, commitment, and failure stage concerning one Protocol State Transition | Every stage MUST preserve the identity of the same authorized `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-008"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition lifecycle representation | Authorization, execution, commitment, or failure MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified first-class transition object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-009"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-009 | MUST | applicable | Every terminal Protocol State Transition classification | A terminal transition MUST be classified as committed or transition failure, but not both. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-010"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every authorized transition that has not reached a terminal classification | Absence of commitment MUST NOT by itself be represented as transition failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transocc-011"></a> PE-G4-TRANSOCC-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition identifier, lifecycle record, representation, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, storage key, and observability surface | A transition-occurrence surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, carry enrollment or admission provenance, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecmpid-001"></a> PE-G4-TSECMPID-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Each comparison attempt MUST bind one successor generation, mapping, profile, authorization, D, Q0 and U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecmpid-002"></a> PE-G4-TSECMPID-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Comparison ordinals MUST begin at 000001 and be contiguous within U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecmpid-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSECMPID-003-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Each attempt and result MUST remain immutable history of U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecmpid-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSECMPID-003-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Each attempt and result MUST have no lifecycle independent of U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-09 / RF-28 — Transition authorization and cardinality

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-001"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-001 | MUST | applicable | Every authorization of a Protocol State Transition arising from a Protocol Action | The transition MUST be authorized by one accepted Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-002"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every rejected Determination, incomplete Evaluation, Evaluation failure, and absence of Determination | The condition MUST NOT authorize an accepted Protocol State Transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-003"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-003 | MUST | applicable | Every transition authorization | Authorization MUST identify the authorizing Determination, authorized `U`, governing `T`, applicable `Q0`, required successor-state relation, and every protocol-defined ordering or dependency. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-004"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-004 | MUST | applicable | Every transition authorization | Authorization MUST select one sufficiently bounded transition occurrence rather than a class of later implementation-selected outcomes. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-005"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every accepted Protocol Action Determination | Acceptance MUST NOT by itself be represented as establishing transition authorization or that a transition occurrence exists. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-006"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition authorization | Authorization MUST NOT be represented as transition execution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-007"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition authorization | Authorization MUST NOT be represented as transition commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-008"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition authorization | Authorization MUST NOT be represented as an authoritative protocol-state change or authoritative successor state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-009"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-009 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted Determination for which transition cardinality is applicable | The complete applicable protocol rules MUST determine whether the Determination authorizes zero, exactly one, or multiple transition occurrences. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-010"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation realizing transition authorization | The implementation MUST NOT choose transition cardinality for convenience, preferred outcome, infrastructure capability, or implementation policy. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-011"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-011 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted Determination requiring zero transitions | The zero-transition authorization relation MUST preserve the successful terminal behavior defined by RF-25 without fabricating a transition occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-012"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-012 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted Determination authorizing multiple independently committable effects | Each independently committable effect MUST remain one separately identified and separately atomic transition occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-013"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-013 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted Determination authorizing multiple transitions with protocol-defined ordering or dependencies | Every authorized ordering and dependency MUST be preserved across the separately identified transitions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-014"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-014 | MUST | applicable | Every group of protocol-required state effects that must succeed or fail together | The effects MUST be represented as one composite atomic Protocol State Transition rather than as independently committable transitions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transauth-015"></a> PE-G4-TRANSAUTH-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every authorization of multiple separately identified Protocol State Transitions | Multiple authorized transitions MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified first-class transition-set object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every authorization MUST use the canonical header and component-binding serialization defined by S71. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | The authorization header MUST bind generation, mapping and digest, profile and digest, authorization, U, D, Q0, successor-state relation, coordinate-set digests and component count. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Ordering and dependency coordinate sets MUST be canonically serialized and digest-bound even when empty. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-004-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-004-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Each component binding mode MUST be fixed by the published profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-004-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-004-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | Each component binding mode MUST NOT be chosen per execution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-005"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-005 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Canonical-value mode MUST bind the exact normalized canonical bytes without implying public disclosure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-006"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-006 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Blinded mode MUST bind authorization context, component identity, canonical type, canonical bytes and one independent 32-octet blind under PE-TSE-SHA256-COMMITMENT-V1. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-007"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-007 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Commitment opening MUST authenticate its source, recompute every bound coordinate and require exact digest equality before semantic comparison. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseauth-008"></a> PE-G4-TSEAUTH-008 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Missing, invalid, unauthenticated or mismatched authorization material MUST yield indeterminate and prohibit execution and commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S71 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-014"></a> PE-G4-CAP-014 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | For this type, an accepting D MUST require one successful scoped unused-to-used effect through a separately valid authorization and U; the accepted no-transition route MUST NOT be selected. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#cardinality | PE-G3-NOTRANS-001, PE-G3-TRANSAUTH-001, PE-G3-TRANSAUTH-002 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-015"></a> PE-G4-CAP-015 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | D alone MUST NOT authorize or perform that effect. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#cardinality | PE-G3-NOTRANS-001, PE-G3-TRANSAUTH-001, PE-G3-TRANSAUTH-002 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-016"></a> PE-G4-CAP-016 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Required successful effect cardinality MUST NOT be read as a commitment liveness guarantee or a restriction on the number of terminally failed attempts allowed by recovery. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#cardinality | PE-G3-NOTRANS-001, PE-G3-TRANSAUTH-001, PE-G3-TRANSAUTH-002 |

## SF-10 / RF-29 — Transition execution and preservation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-001"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition execution | Execution MUST be the bounded implementation activity that attempts to realize one already-authorized `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-002"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-002 | MUST | applicable | Every execution entry | Execution MUST begin only where the applicable authorization and transition-eligibility conditions permit execution of the identified `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-003"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-003 | MUST | applicable | Every execution of an authorized Protocol State Transition | Execution MUST preserve the transition identity, authorizing Determination, governing mapping, authorized pre-transition state, required successor-state relation, atomicity, and complete protocol-defined effects. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-004"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-004 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation executing an authorized Protocol State Transition | The implementation MUST realize the authorized `U` exactly as defined by the published transition semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-005"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation executing an authorized Protocol State Transition | The implementation MUST NOT redefine, reinterpret, widen, narrow, repair, merge, divide, or infer replacement semantics for `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | STATE |
