| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-006"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every execution of authorized `U` | Execution MUST NOT substitute, execute, commit, or report `U'` as `U` where `U' != U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-007"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-007 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between transition eligibility and transition execution | Eligibility and execution MUST remain distinct stages, with eligibility deciding only whether the already-identified transition may proceed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-008"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every completed transition execution | Completed execution MUST NOT by itself be represented as commitment or authoritative protocol-state change. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-009"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every successor-state value or representation derived during execution | Execution machinery and execution output MUST NOT claim or expose derived successor-state material as committed authoritative protocol state before valid RF-30 commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-010"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-010 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation unable to preserve the authorized transition during execution | The implementation MUST fail the execution cleanly while preserving the identity and semantics of the authorized `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-011"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every failed transition execution | Execution failure MUST NOT authorize an alternate transition, alternate successor state, partial commitment, or semantic substitution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-transexec-012"></a> PE-G4-TRANSEXEC-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every execution representation, instruction, transaction, internal call, storage operation, retry, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, and observability surface | An execution surface MUST NOT create another architectural transition identity, identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseexec-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEEXEC-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Bounded execution MUST begin only after the immediately preceding comparison returns equivalent. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseexec-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEEXEC-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | material-conflict or indeterminate before execution MUST prohibit execution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseexec-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEEXEC-003-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Execution failure MUST terminally fail U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseexec-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEEXEC-003-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Execution failure MUST prohibit partial commitment, repair, resumption or alternate successor-state substitution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseexec-004"></a> PE-G4-TSEEXEC-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A later execution attempt MUST use a new U and a valid new authorization selected by the total disposition function. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-10 / RF-30 — Transition commitment, completeness, and atomicity

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-001"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-001 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST be the successful terminal predicate of the same identified `U` that was authorized and executed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-002"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-002 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST derive from one valid accepted Determination and valid authorization of `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-003"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-003 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST follow correct execution that preserves the complete authorized identity and semantics of `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-004"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every committed U authorized against Q0 MUST have produced equivalent under its governing immutable profile at the semantic commitment boundary, after successful bounded execution and without an intervening authoritative protocol-state change. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-005"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-005 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST establish the complete protocol-defined state-change relation and the required successor state `Q1`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-006"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-006 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST establish the absence of omitted required effects and unauthorized additional state effects. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-007"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-007 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST preserve semantic atomicity across every protocol-required effect owned by `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-008"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-008 | MUST | applicable | Every valid authorization and execution context for `U` | Commitment MUST be equivalent to complete state change only within that valid bounded context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-009"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every partial execution of a Protocol State Transition | Partial execution MUST NOT be represented as commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-010"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every partial or incomplete state change | A partial or incomplete state change MUST NOT be represented as commitment or authoritative successor state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-011"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition | A transition MUST NOT be partially committed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-012"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation assertion, report, event, record, diagnostic, or representation concerning commitment | The implementation surface MUST NOT by itself establish protocol commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-commit-013"></a> PE-G4-COMMIT-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every commitment representation and interpretation | Commitment MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified first-class object or as possessing authority or lifecycle independent of `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecommit-001"></a> PE-G4-TSECOMMIT-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Successful execution MUST be followed by authoritative comparison revalidation at the semantic commitment boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecommit-002"></a> PE-G4-TSECOMMIT-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Only equivalent at revalidation MUST permit commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecommit-003"></a> PE-G4-TSECOMMIT-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | material-conflict, indeterminate or revalidation failure after execution MUST terminally fail U and prohibit commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S36 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecommit-004"></a> PE-G4-TSECOMMIT-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | No partial protocol state or alternate successor state from a failed U MUST become authoritative. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsecommit-005"></a> PE-G4-TSECOMMIT-005 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Revalidation and commitment MUST form one indivisible protocol-semantic decision without requiring any implementation transaction mechanism. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-008"></a> PE-G4-CAP-008 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. | <a id="pe-g4-cap-009"></a> PE-G4-CAP-009 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Later root expiry MUST NOT alter an existing D. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S74#observation | PE-G3-TSEEXEC-001, PE-G3-TSEPROF-005 |

| <a id="pe-g4-cap-017"></a> PE-G4-CAP-017 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. | <a id="pe-g4-cap-018"></a> PE-G4-CAP-018 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | It MUST preserve all other keys, membership state/history and product state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#successor-relation | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEEXEC-003-A02 |

All baseline antecedents preserved. | It MUST NOT restore unrelated state from an earlier snapshot or commit the same key again under the unused precondition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#successor-relation | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEEXEC-003-A02 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-020"></a> PE-G4-CAP-020 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Final comparison and semantic commitment MUST remain indivisible with no intervening authoritative state change. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#successor-relation | PE-G3-TSEPROF-005, PE-G3-TSEEXEC-003-A02 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-048"></a> PE-G4-CAP-048 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | E observations MUST bind fixed E/C; each gate observation MUST independently bind the exact authorization, D, Q0, mapping/profile and digests, fixed semantic basis, gate stage and comparison attempt of that U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#gates | PE-G3-TSECMPID-001, PE-G3-TSEEXEC-001 |

## SF-10 / RF-31 — Protocol state, successor state, and state evolution

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-state-001"></a> PE-G4-STATE-001 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol-state representation | Protocol state MUST contain only state governed as protocol state by the applicable published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | STATE, PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-002"></a> PE-G4-STATE-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application, implementation-local, transport, database, cache, UI, operational, diagnostic, and observability state representation | The representation MUST NOT be treated as protocol state merely because it describes, realizes, stores, or observes protocol activity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-003"></a> PE-G4-STATE-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition authorization | The authorization MUST identify the bounded pre-transition protocol state `Q0` against which `U` is authorized. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-004"></a> PE-G4-STATE-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation realizing an authorized transition | The implementation MUST NOT select, reconstruct, repair, or substitute a preferred pre-transition protocol state after authorization. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-005"></a> PE-G4-STATE-005 | MUST | applicable | Every successor-state derivation | `Q1` MUST be derived only from the authorized `U`, governing `T`, applicable `Q0`, and complete published transition semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-006"></a> PE-G4-STATE-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation deriving successor-state material | The implementation MUST NOT invent, infer, repair, merge, widen, narrow, substitute, or add application meaning to `Q1`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-007"></a> PE-G4-STATE-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every successor-state value derived before commitment | Derived successor-state material MUST NOT be represented as authoritative protocol state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-008"></a> PE-G4-STATE-008 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Only valid commitment of `U` MUST establish `Q1` as authoritative successor state for that transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-009"></a> PE-G4-STATE-009 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition from `Q0` to `Q1` | The pre-transition state, successor state, and transition occurrence MUST retain distinct identities and their exact committed relationship. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-010"></a> PE-G4-STATE-010 | MUST | applicable | Every authoritative protocol-state evolution | State evolution MUST consist of one applicable `Q0`, one committed `U`, and the protocol-defined authoritative `Q1`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-011"></a> PE-G4-STATE-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every failed, incomplete, partial, simulated, predicted, or merely reported transition | The transition condition MUST NOT establish authoritative state evolution or authoritative `Q1`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-012"></a> PE-G4-STATE-012 | MUST | applicable | Every committed protocol-state evolution | The evolution MUST preserve every applicable published protocol invariant and every complete protocol-required effect of `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35, PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-state-013"></a> PE-G4-STATE-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every protocol-state identifier, state representation, successor-state derivation, evolution record, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, storage key, and observability surface | A protocol-state surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, carry enrollment or admission provenance, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35, PE-G4-SOURCE-S38, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsestate-001"></a> PE-G4-TSESTATE-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Current comparison values MUST be obtained only from authenticated protocol-owned authoritative state sources declared by the profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsestate-002"></a> PE-G4-TSESTATE-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | Runtime, application, database, diagnostic and observability state MUST NOT substitute for authoritative protocol state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsestate-003"></a> PE-G4-TSESTATE-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | State identifiers, ordering coordinates and dependency coordinates MUST be authenticated and canonicalized before predicate evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsestate-004"></a> PE-G4-TSESTATE-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | State evolution and successor-state establishment MUST occur only through a committed U whose final comparison returned equivalent. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S38, PE-G4-SOURCE-S39 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-011"></a> PE-G4-CAP-011 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | For the selected capability, the owned state MUST be the scoped tuple (a,n,used), with canonical scalar a,n and authoritative used in {FALSE,TRUE}. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#source-successor-classes | PE-G3-UNIQ-001, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-012"></a> PE-G4-CAP-012 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | The eligible source class MUST be exactly (a,n,FALSE), and the bounded successor class MUST be exactly (a,n,TRUE). | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#source-successor-classes | PE-G3-UNIQ-001, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-013"></a> PE-G4-CAP-013 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Missing or unresolved state MUST NOT be treated as unused. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S75#source-successor-classes | PE-G3-UNIQ-001, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-039"></a> PE-G4-CAP-039 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Each root and uniqueness observation MUST identify its exact owning source meaning and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#scope | PE-G3-RULE-003, PE-G3-RULE-008, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-040"></a> PE-G4-CAP-040 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Their association to the same selected capability, fixed PI, type and context MUST be validated. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#scope | PE-G3-RULE-003, PE-G3-RULE-008, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-041"></a> PE-G4-CAP-041 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A provider label, wallet, product record or equal tuple alone MUST NOT establish that association. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#scope | PE-G3-RULE-003, PE-G3-RULE-008, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-042"></a> PE-G4-CAP-042 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A cut MUST denote the bounded source-authoritative observation boundary at which the asserted facts hold. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#cut | PE-G3-TSEPROF-004, PE-G3-TSEAUTH-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-043"></a> PE-G4-CAP-043 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Its identifier MUST NOT itself establish authenticity, completeness, currentness, finality or cross-source ordering. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#cut | PE-G3-TSEPROF-004, PE-G3-TSEAUTH-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-044"></a> PE-G4-CAP-044 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Root evidence MUST establish the complete count and newest valid suffix; state evidence MUST establish the complete exact scoped-key state at the applicable boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#cut | PE-G3-TSEPROF-004, PE-G3-TSEAUTH-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-045"></a> PE-G4-CAP-045 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | The owning source evidence MUST establish that individually validated root/state observations can be used together under the fixed context at the requested phase without incompatible authoritative states or omission of a relevant change. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#joint | PE-G3-EVAL-005, PE-G3-PROOF-007, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-046"></a> PE-G4-CAP-046 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Joint applicability MUST NOT be inferred from individual signatures, equal cuts or plausible values alone. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#joint | PE-G3-EVAL-005, PE-G3-PROOF-007, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-047"></a> PE-G4-CAP-047 | MUST NOT | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. | <a id="pe-g4-cap-059"></a> PE-G4-CAP-059 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. | <a id="pe-g4-cap-060"></a> PE-G4-CAP-060 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | A missing record MUST mean unused only if a competent complete-state rule establishes that interpretation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S77#state | PE-G3-UNIQ-001, PE-G3-TSEPROF-004 |

## SF-11 / RF-32 — Protocol-state history

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-001"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol State Transition included as a committed state change in authoritative protocol-state history | The transition MUST have validly committed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-002"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-002 | MUST | applicable | Every authoritative protocol-state history representation | The representation MUST preserve each committed transition identity, committed ordering, applicable pre-transition state, authoritative successor state, and bounded public consequence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-003"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every failed Protocol State Transition | A failed transition MUST NOT be included as a committed transition in authoritative protocol-state history. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-004"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every pre-transition failure | A pre-transition failure MUST NOT be included as a committed transition in authoritative protocol-state history. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-005"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every partial execution or partial state change | Partial execution or partial state change MUST NOT be included as committed history. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-006"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every operational attempt, diagnostic, retry, log, database row, event, report, error, cache record, or observability record | An operational record MUST NOT be treated as authoritative protocol-state history merely because it describes protocol activity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-007"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-007 | MUST | applicable | Every permitted protocol-state history realization | The realization MUST preserve identical committed transition semantics independently of storage structure, database, blockchain, consensus, event, transaction, or infrastructure choice. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-statehist-008"></a> PE-G4-STATEHIST-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every history identifier, entry, representation, ordering record, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, storage key, and observability surface | A protocol-state history surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |

## SF-11 / RF-33 — Public protocol-state consequences

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-001"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-001 | MUST | applicable | Every committed Protocol State Transition | Commitment MUST establish the bounded public protocol-state consequence defined for `U` and authoritative `Q1`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-002"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-002 | MUST | applicable | Every public protocol-state consequence | The consequence MUST remain bounded to the published protocol meaning of the committed state change. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-003"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-003 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Published Result and a public protocol-state consequence | The interface-level Published Result and committed protocol-state consequence MUST remain distinct semantic roles. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-004"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-004 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between a Protocol Action Determination and a public protocol-state consequence | The Determination and committed consequence MUST remain distinct, including where both are publicly observable. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-005"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-005 | MUST | applicable | Every relationship between authoritative successor state and public protocol-state consequence | The successor-state representation and its bounded public consequence MUST remain distinct without introducing an intermediate consequence object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-006"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public protocol-state consequence and every interpretation of it | The consequence MUST NOT be represented as application, factual, legal, social, institutional, user-intent, or off-protocol truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-007"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public protocol-state consequence | The consequence MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified first-class object possessing authority or lifecycle independent of committed `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-008"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public consequence identifier, representation, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, storage key, and observability surface | A public-consequence surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-stateconseq-009"></a> PE-G4-STATECONSEQ-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every public consequence recording authorization, membership, eligibility, uniqueness, possession, or another bounded protocol property | The recorded property MUST NOT be extended into actor identification, enrollment or admission provenance, hidden intent, or additional correlation meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |

## SF-11 / RF-34 — Failure classifications and failure separation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-001"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-001 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol-use failure or implementation-error classification | The classification MUST identify the actual bounded lifecycle stage and condition that occurred. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-002"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-002 | MUST | applicable | Every pre-evaluation failure | Pre-evaluation failure MUST mean that no valid Protocol Action Evaluation occurrence began. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-003"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action Evaluation failure | Evaluation failure MUST mean that one Evaluation began but did not complete and produced no Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-004"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action rejection | Rejection MUST remain the rejecting classification of one valid Determination produced by one completed Evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-005"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-005 | MUST | applicable | Every accepted no-transition terminal path | Accepted no-transition MUST remain a successful Determination-boundary completion rather than a failure classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-006"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-006 | MUST | applicable | Every pre-transition failure | Pre-transition failure MUST mean that the post-Determination transition boundary could not validly enter bounded execution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-007"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-007 | MUST | applicable | Every transition failure | Transition failure MUST remain the explicit terminal classification of an authorized transition that does not establish complete commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-008"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every stale authorization | Stale authorization MUST NOT be represented as rejection, Evaluation failure, or proof that the earlier Determination was incorrect. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-009"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-009 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation error | Implementation error MUST remain a bounded diagnosis of incorrect realization, classification, commitment, or reporting rather than a redefinition of protocol truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-010"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-010 | MUST | applicable | Every incorrect execution diagnosis | Incorrect execution MUST identify implementation failure to preserve or correctly realize authorized `U` without becoming semantically equivalent to every transition failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-011"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-011 | MUST | applicable | Every incorrect commitment diagnosis | Incorrect commitment MUST identify an implementation representation or materialization that does not satisfy valid protocol commitment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-012"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-012 | MUST | applicable | Every incorrect reporting diagnosis | Incorrect reporting MUST remain distinct from the protocol condition incorrectly reported. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-013"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every pre-transition failure | Pre-transition failure MUST NOT be represented as transition failure where bounded execution of `U` did not begin. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-014"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition failure, stale authorization, implementation error, incorrect execution, incorrect commitment, and incorrect reporting condition | A later-stage condition MUST NOT be converted into Protocol Action rejection or Evaluation failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-015"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every infrastructure failure, inability to preserve `U`, changed-state condition, partial execution, or reporting error | The condition MUST NOT be silently converted into commitment or another semantically different success or failure class. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-016"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-016 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every failure, error, staleness, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, storage key, and observability surface | A failure surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, expose implementation or operational metadata as actor identity, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-017"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-017 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every failure classification and implementation-error diagnosis | A failure classification or error diagnosis MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified first-class failure object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-018"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-018 | MUST | applicable | Every failure before authoritative Evaluation begins | Failure before authoritative Evaluation begins MUST create no authoritative Evaluation, Determination, acceptance or rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-019"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-019 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every later failure after Determination production | Failure of representation, serialization, persistence, publication, transport, operational delivery, display, indexing or application translation after Determination production MUST NOT erase the Determination, reopen or fail the completed Evaluation, alter its acceptance or rejection classification, or become protocol rejection. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-fail-020"></a> PE-G4-FAIL-020 | MUST | applicable | Every operational success or failure classification | Operational success or failure MUST remain distinct from Determination production and from formal Evaluation success or failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsefail-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEFAIL-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An unavailable, invalid, unauthenticated, incomplete or unevaluable required input MUST be classified UNRESOLVED rather than FALSE. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsefail-002-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEFAIL-002-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An authoritatively evaluated false condition MUST remain FALSE. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsefail-002-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEFAIL-002-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An authoritatively evaluated false condition MUST be processed by the profile's materiality definition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S37 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsefail-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEFAIL-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | An independently sufficient authenticated material-conflict witness MUST yield material-conflict despite an unrelated unresolved input. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsefail-004"></a> PE-G4-TSEFAIL-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Without an established sufficient conflict, any unresolved input required for equivalence or conflict exclusion MUST yield indeterminate. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsefail-005"></a> PE-G4-TSEFAIL-005 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Complete valid inputs producing neither equivalence nor conflict, or both, MUST yield indeterminate and invalidate the profile or evaluation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-11 / RF-35 — Stale authorization and re-evaluation

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-001"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-001 | MUST | applicable | Every transition authorization bounded to one pre-transition protocol state | The authorization MUST remain explicitly bounded to the identified `Q0`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-002"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every authorized U MUST execute the PE-TSE-COMPARISON-V2 procedure immediately before execution and again at semantic commitment, using authenticated authorization bindings and authoritative current protocol state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-003-A01 | MUST | conditional | When comparison returns equivalent, | When comparison returns equivalent, the equivalence determination MUST remain bounded to U and its governing profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-003-A02 | MUST | conditional | When comparison returns equivalent, | When comparison returns equivalent, the equivalence determination MUST preserve D and the authorization unchanged. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-003-a03"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-003-A03 | MUST | conditional | When comparison returns equivalent, | When comparison returns equivalent, the equivalence determination MUST authorize only the next gate for that U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-004-a01"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-004-A01 | MUST | conditional | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, U MUST terminate with pre-transition failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-004-a02"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-004-A02 | MUST | conditional | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, U's authorization MUST become terminally stale for U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-004-a03"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-004-A03 | MUST | conditional | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, execution and commitment MUST be prohibited. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-004-a04"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-004-A04 | MUST | conditional | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, | When pre-execution comparison returns material-conflict, recovery MUST require a new U under the total disposition function. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-005"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every stale transition authorization | A stale authorization MUST NOT permit commitment against the materially conflicting state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-006"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation resolving applicable-state conflict | The implementation MUST NOT select whichever state context produces a preferred result or repair the existing authorization. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-007"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every stale transition authorization | Staleness MUST NOT mutate, invalidate, rewrite, or reclassify the earlier Evaluation or Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-008"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-008 | MUST | applicable | Every materially changed context or state for which the applicable protocol rules require re-evaluation | The implementation MUST begin one new Protocol Action Evaluation occurrence under the newly applicable bounded context. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-009"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-009 | MUST | applicable | Every completed required re-evaluation | The new Evaluation MUST produce one new Determination and, where applicable, newly authorized transition occurrences whose identities are distinct from every earlier transition occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-010"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every required re-evaluation | Re-evaluation MUST NOT mutate, repair, replace, or silently reinterpret the earlier `E`, `D`, or `U`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-011"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every stale-authorization and re-evaluation identifier, record, diagnostic, log, field, route, test, example, serialization, storage key, and observability surface | A staleness or re-evaluation surface MUST NOT identify the Private Actor, introduce reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-012"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every stale-authorization condition and representation | Stale authorization MUST NOT be represented as a separately identified first-class stale-authorization object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-013"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-013 | MUST | applicable | Every retry of later Determination representation, publication, transport or delivery | Retry of a later representation, publication, transport or delivery operation MUST preserve the same already-produced Determination and MUST NOT produce another Determination or another Evaluation occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-014"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every terminally failed Protocol Action Evaluation | A terminally failed Evaluation MUST NOT later become complete. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-staleauth-015"></a> PE-G4-STALEAUTH-015 | MUST | applicable | Every later authoritative re-evaluation after terminal Evaluation failure | Any later authoritative re-evaluation MUST instantiate a new Evaluation occurrence and MUST preserve the failed occurrence without mutation, repair or reinterpretation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-001-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-001-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Dep(D) MUST be the validated least transitive closure defined by S70. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-001-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-001-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Dep(D) MUST include every input capable of affecting D. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-002"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Unchanged D MUST be sufficient only when its complete dependency closure remains authoritative, valid, authenticated and semantically unchanged. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-003"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every changed or unavailable component MUST be outside Dep(D) before recovery may reuse unchanged D. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-004"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-004 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | The stage-partitioned disposition function in S72 MUST select exactly one recovery for every non-success event. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-005"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-005 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Pre-execution material conflict MUST terminally fail U, terminally stale its authorization and require a new U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-006"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-006 | MUST | conditional | When unchanged D is insufficient or cannot be established, | When unchanged D is insufficient or cannot be established, recovery MUST require new E, successfully produced new D, new authorization and new U. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tserecov-007"></a> PE-G4-TSERECOV-007 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every predecessor occurrence, authorization, attempt, result and failure classification MUST remain immutable through recovery. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-030"></a> PE-G4-CAP-030 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | For every unchanged recovery row requiring SAME E and SAME D, the existing A/E/D MUST be preserved. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#recovery | PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A01, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A02, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-002 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-031"></a> PE-G4-CAP-031 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Where a row requires NEW E, a new authoritative admission MUST establish a new A before that E and a new D MUST arise only on successful completion. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#recovery | PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A01, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A02, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-002 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-032"></a> PE-G4-CAP-032 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | Failed U MUST remain terminal. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#recovery | PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A01, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A02, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-002 |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-033"></a> PE-G4-CAP-033 | MUST | conditional | Selected anonymous-membership participation capability at the phase specified by this sentence; conditional realization details apply only when that adapter is selected. All baseline antecedents preserved. | No recovery overlay MUST change the 21-row function or authorize a second consumption of a used key. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S76#recovery | PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A01, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-001-A02, PE-G3-TSEREEVAL-002 |

## SF-12 / RF-14 — Application requests, responses, and adapters

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-001"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Application Request | An Application Request MUST remain a bounded application-owned representation presented for possible translation at one identified application boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-002"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-002 | MUST | applicable | Every identified Application Request | The request identity and scope MUST identify the request representation without identifying a Private Actor or becoming a reusable cross-action identifier. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-003"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Application Request | An Application Request MUST NOT be interpreted merely by its existence as a Protocol Operation, Protocol Action, proof, declared public input, Evaluation, Determination, Protocol State Transition, protocol state, or protocol truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S23, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-004"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-004 | MUST | applicable | Every application-owned request value | Each request value MUST remain application-owned until a valid adapter translation establishes its applicable protocol-facing representation under the governing Interface Contract. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-005"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Application Adapter | An Application Adapter MUST remain a bounded translation boundary between application-owned representation and the applicable protocol-facing interface representation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-006"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-006 | MUST | applicable | Every identified Application Adapter | Adapter identity and scope MUST remain distinct from the application, runtime, implementation, Interface Contract, Concrete Interface Specification, Protocol Operation, Protocol Action, and Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-007"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-007 | MUST | applicable | Every adapter translation | Translation MUST be governed by the applicable Interface Contract and Concrete Interface Specification and MUST map only representations supported by that boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S31, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-008"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-008 | MUST | applicable | Every successful adapter translation | A successful translation MUST preserve the complete bounded meaning, identity distinctions, ordering requirements, applicability conditions, and failure semantics of its source representation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-009"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-009 | MUST | applicable | Every adapter unable to preserve applicable meaning | The adapter MUST return bounded translation failure when it cannot preserve the applicable meaning or required representation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-010"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-010 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every adapter translation | Translation MUST NOT repair, infer, widen, narrow, merge, divide, substitute, or invent protocol meaning, protocol subjects, protocol values, or protocol outcomes. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-011"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-011 | MUST | applicable | Every Application Response | An Application Response MUST remain a bounded application-owned representation derived from one applicable protocol-defined output exposed through the governing interface. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-012"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-012 | MUST | applicable | Every identified Application Response | Response identity and scope MUST identify the response representation without changing the identity of its source output or identifying a Private Actor. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-013"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Application Response | An Application Response MUST NOT be identified with or substituted for its source Published Result, public protocol-state consequence, Determination, protocol state, or application interpretation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-014"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-014 | MUST | applicable | Every Application Response derived through translation | The response MUST preserve the complete bounded meaning and failure status of its source protocol-defined output while remaining application-owned representation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-015"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Application Request, adapter output, and Application Response | Application-facing representation MUST NOT convert protocol output into factual, legal, social, institutional, or application truth or into evidence of user intent or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-apprep-016"></a> PE-G4-APPREP-016 | MUST | applicable | Every request, adapter, response, field, serialization, example, and diagnostic surface | Application-facing representation and translation surfaces MUST preserve SF-03 and SF-04 terminology, identity separation, privacy, unlinkability, minimum-disclosure, and neutral-naming requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | STATE |

## SF-12 / RF-36 — Application and adapter authority boundaries

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-001"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-001 | MAY | applicable | Every application | An application MAY define application-local representation, terminology, policy, validation, presentation, persistence, and interpretation within its declared application-owned scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-002"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application policy or interpretation | Application policy or interpretation MUST NOT alter protocol validity, applicable protocol obligations, proof meaning, public-input meaning, Evaluation, Determination, transition semantics, commitment, protocol state, compatibility, conformance, or certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-003"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application and Application Request or Response | An application or its representations MUST NOT acquire protocol authority or represent application-owned meaning as protocol-defined meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-004"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Application Adapter | An adapter MUST exercise responsibility only for bounded representation handling and translation within its governing interface boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-005"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Application Adapter | An adapter MUST NOT redefine Interface Contract meaning, Protocol Surface meaning, Protocol Operation meaning, Protocol Action identity, proof or public-input meaning, Evaluation, Determination, transition, commitment, state, Published Result, public consequence, validity, privacy, compatibility, conformance, or certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-006"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-006 | MUST | applicable | Every integrator | An integrator MUST preserve every applicable upstream semantic subject, requirement, identity distinction, ordering rule, failure rule, privacy boundary, and authority boundary across the connected application, adapter, runtime, and implementation surfaces. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-007"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every integration | Integration MUST NOT authorize semantic repair, inference, substitution, correlation, authority transfer, or the creation of an application-specific alternative protocol path. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-008"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-008 | MUST | applicable | Every adapter replacement or application-boundary change | The replacement or change MUST preserve every applicable published interface meaning and MUST fail compatibility where that meaning cannot be preserved. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-009"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application-side validation or default | Application validation, defaults, convenience abstractions, or preferred outcomes MUST NOT be treated as protocol validation, protocol defaults, protocol rules, or protocol outcomes. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-010"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-010 | MUST | applicable | Every application, adapter, and integration failure | Failure MUST be reported within its bounded layer and MUST preserve the distinction between representation failure, runtime failure, implementation failure, and protocol-defined failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-011"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application, adapter, integration record, or observability surface | The surface MUST NOT identify a Private Actor, carry enrollment or admission provenance, create reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or compose metadata into an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-appauth-012"></a> PE-G4-APPAUTH-012 | MUST | applicable | Every public application, adapter, or integration identifier | The identifier MUST remain bounded to its declared non-actor subject without being interpreted as protocol identity, Protocol Action identity, or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseapp-001-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEAPP-001-A01 | MAY | applicable | ALWAYS | An application response MAY translate the authoritative bounded result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseapp-001-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEAPP-001-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | An application response MUST NOT create, change or reinterpret the authoritative bounded result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S42 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseapp-002"></a> PE-G4-TSEAPP-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | An application surface MUST NOT receive or expose confidential blinds, openings or raw protected components. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S42, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |

## SF-12 / RF-37 — Runtime, enforcement, and execution responsibility

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-001"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine runtime | A runtime MUST remain an identified operational context through which one or more identified implementations perform bounded protocol-facing work. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-002"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-002 | MUST | applicable | Every identified runtime | Runtime identity and scope MUST remain distinct from application identity, adapter identity, implementation identity, deployment identity, protocol identity, Protocol Action identity, and Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-003"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every runtime | A runtime MUST NOT interpret application-local meaning as protocol meaning or assume application ownership merely because it receives application-facing material. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-004"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-004 | MUST | applicable | Every runtime using an implementation | The runtime MUST preserve the identity and declared scope of each implementation through which it realizes protocol-facing work. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-005"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-005 | MAY | applicable | Every runtime | A runtime MAY receive applicable inputs, invoke validation and enforcement, invoke Evaluation, invoke authorized execution, expose applicable results, and report bounded failure where those responsibilities are assigned by the applicable implementation profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-006"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every runtime orchestration path | Runtime orchestration order, component topology, retry behavior, transport, storage, or deployment mechanics MUST NOT redefine the semantic identity or ordering requirements of Protocol Actions, Evaluations, Determinations, transitions, commitment, state, history, or consequences. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-007"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Enforcement occurrence | Enforcement MUST evaluate only the applicable published transition preconditions and MUST contribute its bounded eligibility result to the complete applicable Protocol Action Evaluation where required. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-008"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Enforcement occurrence | Enforcement MUST NOT be interpreted as the complete Protocol Action Evaluation or as an independent Protocol Action Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-009"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Enforcement occurrence | Enforcement MUST NOT execute a transition, commit state, construct authoritative successor state, publish a result, or select an alternative transition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-010"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-010 | MUST | applicable | Every authorized transition execution | Execution MUST preserve the already-authorized Protocol State Transition occurrence and realize only its published transition mapping. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-011"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every transition execution | Execution MUST NOT perform enforcement, substitute another transition occurrence, or treat derived successor-state material as committed authoritative state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-012"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every successful execution | Successful execution MUST NOT be interpreted as commitment until the complete SF-10 commitment requirements have been satisfied. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-013"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-013 | MUST | applicable | Every runtime result or failure report | Runtime reporting MUST preserve the source result, lifecycle stage, success or failure classification, and applicable identity distinctions without inventing protocol truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-014"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every runtime or implementation failure | A runtime or implementation failure MUST NOT mutate an earlier Protocol Action, Evaluation, Determination, authorization, transition occurrence, commitment status, or protocol-state history. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-015"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-015 | MUST | applicable | Every runtime value, log, diagnostic, retry, timing, ordering, topology, and observability surface | The surface MUST preserve SF-04 privacy without exposing or composing runtime metadata as Private Actor identity or cross-action linkage. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-016"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-016 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every runtime, enforcement mechanism, execution mechanism, and orchestrator | Operational responsibility MUST NOT be interpreted as ownership of protocol semantics or as protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S33, PE-G4-SOURCE-S34, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-runtime-017"></a> PE-G4-RUNTIME-017 | MUST | applicable | Every operational failure after a Determination exists | Operational failure after a Determination exists MUST be classified at the actual later lifecycle layer. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-001-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-001-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A runtime MUST implement the published profile and gate exactly. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-001-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-001-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A runtime MUST NOT choose predicates, defaults, materiality or recovery. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-002"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-002 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | A runtime comparison boundary handling openings MUST authenticate inputs, minimize disclosure and erase non-required opening material after the bounded comparison. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S43 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-003-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-003-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Operational failure MUST be classified at its actual lifecycle layer. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-003-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-003-A02 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | Operational failure MUST NOT become protocol rejection, material conflict or equivalence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-004-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-004-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Runtime retry MUST preserve immutable history. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S47, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tseruntime-004-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSERUNTIME-004-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Runtime retry MUST instantiate new occurrences exactly where the disposition function requires them. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S47, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-13 / RF-38 — Implementation identity and implementation obligations

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-001"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine implementation | An implementation MUST be one identified technical realization of the applicable normative requirements selected by its applicable implementation profile or profiles. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-002"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-002 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation | Implementation identity MUST be stable and sufficient to distinguish the implementation from other implementations and materially different implementation versions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-003"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-003 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation | The implementation MUST declare a bounded realization scope identifying the roles, capabilities, interfaces, protocol generation, Protocol Specification, and implementation profiles it claims to realize. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-004"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-004 | MUST | applicable | Every materially changed implementation version | A materially changed version MUST remain separately identifiable where the change affects applicable obligations, externally observable normative behavior, compatibility, or assessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-005"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-005 | MAY | applicable | Every implementation | An implementation MAY use multiple components, algorithms, languages, processes, services, storage structures, transports, and deployment mechanisms where every applicable requirement permits that variation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-006"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-006 | MUST | applicable | Every component used by an implementation | Component identity, responsibility, and failure MUST remain attributable to the implementation scope without becoming independent protocol identity or protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-007"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-007 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation deployment or operational instance | Deployment and instance identity MUST remain distinct from implementation identity, implementation-version identity, profile identity, protocol identity, and Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-008"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation identifier | An implementation identifier MUST NOT be interpreted as a protocol-generation identifier, Protocol Specification identifier, implementation-profile identifier, reference designation, conformance identity, certification identity, Protocol Action identity, or Private Actor identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-009"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-009 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation | The implementation MUST satisfy every normative requirement applicable through its identified profile, including applicable success, rejection, failure, privacy, observability, and authority requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-010"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-010 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation claiming a requirement is non-applicable | The claim MUST derive from the requirement's published applicability rule and the applicable profile scope rather than implementation preference or existing behavior. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-011"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-011 | MAY | applicable | Every implementation-facing choice explicitly permitted by the applicable profile | The implementation MAY select a choice only within the complete bounded choice space permitted by the Protocol Specification and resolved or retained by the applicable profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-012"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation | Implementation convenience, cost, infrastructure limitation, preferred result, deployment topology, storage model, transport model, local policy, or existing behavior MUST NOT become protocol discretion or an exception to an applicable requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-013"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-013 | MAY | applicable | Multiple implementations of the same applicable profile | Implementations MAY differ technically where every difference remains within permitted implementation choice and preserves equivalent protocol-observable semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-014"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-014 | MUST | applicable | Every permitted implementation variation | The variation MUST preserve every applicable requirement, semantic subject, identity distinction, ordering rule, failure behavior, privacy guarantee, and observable protocol outcome. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-015"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-015 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation failure | Failure MUST remain explicit, bounded to the failed realization responsibility, and distinguishable from protocol invalidity and from alteration of the governing requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-impl-016"></a> PE-G4-IMPL-016 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation name, identifier, version, component, deployment, instance, log, diagnostic, test, and observability surface | The surface MUST NOT identify a Private Actor, create reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or compose implementation metadata into an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | STATE |

## SF-13 / RF-39 — Protocol realization

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-001"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-001 | MUST | applicable | Every claimed Protocol Realization | The realization MUST identify one implementation, one applicable Protocol Specification and published protocol generation, the applicable implementation profile or profiles, and one bounded realization scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-002"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Realization relationship | The relationship MUST remain a bounded preservation relation between its identified implementation and upstream semantic target rather than a new source of protocol semantics or protocol identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-003"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-003 | MUST | applicable | Every implementation realizing an applicable requirement | The implementation MUST preserve the complete meaning, strength, applicability, identity distinctions, conditions, success behavior, and failure behavior of that requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-004"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-004 | MUST | applicable | Every successful realization of an upstream semantic subject | Every later realization layer MUST preserve the same selected subject `T` throughout the declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-005"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-005 | MUST | applicable | Every realization unable to preserve an applicable subject or requirement | The realization MUST fail explicitly rather than claim successful realization. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-006"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Realization | A realization MUST NOT repair, reinterpret, widen, narrow, merge, divide, substitute, infer, or invent replacement protocol meaning `T'` for the applicable meaning `T`. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-007"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Protocol Realization | A realization MUST NOT omit an applicable mandatory requirement or treat a prohibited behavior as a permitted technical choice. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-008"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-008 | MUST | applicable | Every complete realization claim | The claim MUST account for every requirement applicable within its declared scope and for every declared dependency of the applicable profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-009"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-009 | MAY | applicable | Multiple realizations of the same applicable profile | Multiple realizations MAY use different internal and operational means where each independently preserves the same required protocol-observable semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-010"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-010 | MUST | applicable | Every realization-specific extension | The extension MUST remain outside protocol semantics without conflicting with, weakening, obscuring, or expanding any applicable protocol obligation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-011"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-011 | MUST | applicable | Every realization record, report, or diagnostic | The surface MUST remain bounded to realization status and MUST preserve SF-04 privacy, neutral naming, and non-correlation requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-realize-012"></a> PE-G4-REALIZE-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation and realization | Realizing protocol semantics MUST NOT be interpreted as defining the protocol generation, Protocol Specification, implementation profile, conformance, certification, or protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |

## SF-13 / RF-40 — Prohibited implementation, runtime, application, and assessment authority

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-001"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-001 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every application, adapter, runtime, integration, implementation, realization, compatibility structure, profile, reference implementation, assessment, report, deployment, and operational surface | A downstream structure or surface MUST NOT acquire or claim protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
