| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-002"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream structure or surface | It MUST NOT redefine, replace, amend, supersede, or mutate the identity, membership, publication state, lineage, lifecycle, semantics, or authority of a published protocol generation or Protocol Specification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-003"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream structure or surface | It MUST NOT redefine privacy, anonymity, unlinkability, minimum disclosure, actor separation, reusable-identity prohibitions, or compositional-correlation prohibitions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-004"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream structure or surface | It MUST NOT redefine proof relations, declared public inputs, verification semantics, evidence meaning, or complete protocol validity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-005"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream structure or surface | It MUST NOT redefine Protocol Operation, Protocol Action, applicable-rule, Evaluation, Determination, acceptance, rejection, or accepted no-transition semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S21, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-006"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream structure or surface | It MUST NOT redefine transition mapping, transition-occurrence identity, authorization, execution, commitment, state, history, consequence, failure, staleness, or re-evaluation semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S35, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-007"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every compatibility, profile, reference, conformance, or certification structure | It MUST NOT redefine compatibility truth, profile obligations, conformance, certification, or the upstream semantic basis against which those downstream relationships are evaluated. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-008"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-008 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation behavior, compatibility declaration, assessment result, diagnostic, test result, deployment record, report, or observability surface | The surface MUST NOT be promoted into protocol truth or used to repair, infer, or replace upstream protocol meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-009"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-009 | MUST | applicable | Every conflict between a downstream artifact and its governing upstream requirement | The conflict MUST be resolved in favor of the governing upstream requirement, and the downstream artifact MUST be corrected, narrowed, withdrawn, superseded, or reported as failed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-downauth-010"></a> PE-G4-DOWNAUTH-010 | MUST | applicable | Every downstream derivation and realization path | The path MUST preserve the direction `Published Protocol Generation -> Protocol Specification -> Implementation Profile -> Implementation -> Conformance -> Certification` without upward authority transfer. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | PE-G3-COMPOSITION-MAP-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsenonauth-001"></a> PE-G4-TSENONAUTH-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | No implementation, runtime, adapter, application, database, diagnostic or observability mechanism MUST acquire authority to define or override transition-state equivalence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsenonauth-002"></a> PE-G4-TSENONAUTH-002 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | Protocol-semantic atomicity MUST NOT be inferred from implementation transaction, storage, consensus, lock, event or rollback behaviour. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S36, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## SF-13 / RF-41 — Compatibility targets, surfaces, statements, profiles, declarations, and assessment

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-001"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-001 | MUST | applicable | Every compatibility relationship | The relationship MUST identify one implementation, one implementation version where relevant, one compatibility target, one compatibility surface, and one bounded compatibility scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-002"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Target | A Compatibility Target MUST identify exactly one published protocol generation used as the reference for the bounded compatibility relationship. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-003"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-003 | MUST | applicable | Every identified Compatibility Target | Target identity MUST remain distinct from the identity of the referenced protocol generation, implementation, deployment, surface, statement, profile, declaration, assessment, conformance object, and certification object. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-004"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Surface | A Compatibility Surface MUST identify the bounded set of published capabilities from one identified compatibility target that is covered by the compatibility statement or assessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-005"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Surface | The surface MUST be complete within its declared scope and MUST explicitly identify every included capability and every permitted exclusion. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-006"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Compatibility Surface | A Compatibility Surface MUST NOT be identified with a Protocol Surface, silently omit an applicable declared capability, or imply compatibility outside its bounded scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S22, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-007"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Statement | A Compatibility Statement MUST be a bounded assertion by or about one identified implementation concerning one identified target and one identified surface. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-008"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Statement | The statement MUST identify its implementation, implementation version where relevant, target, surface, declared scope, claim boundary, and statement identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-009"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Compatibility Statement | A statement MUST NOT be interpreted as proof of realization, proof of compatibility, conformance, certification, protocol succession, or protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-010"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Profile | A Compatibility Profile MUST remain a bounded aggregation of identified Compatibility Statements associated with one identified implementation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-011"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-011 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Profile | The profile MUST preserve the identity, target, surface, scope, and asserted meaning of each aggregated statement without merging their target or statement identities. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-012"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Compatibility Profile | A Compatibility Profile MUST NOT be treated as an Implementation Profile, MUST NOT select normative implementation obligations, and MUST NOT establish compatibility merely by aggregation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-013"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-013 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Declaration | A Compatibility Declaration MUST remain the complete identified record of one declared compatibility relationship concerning one implementation, one target, one surface, and one bounded scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-014"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-014 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Declaration | The declaration MUST preserve the complete constituent identities, statement provenance, declared scope, version information, and declared compatibility meaning required by its governing compatibility model. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-015"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-015 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Compatibility Declaration | Publication or persistence of a declaration MUST NOT convert declared compatibility into realized compatibility, protocol truth, conformance, or certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-016"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-016 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Assessment | A Compatibility Assessment MUST evaluate only the identified declared compatibility relationship against its identified target, surface, scope, applicable protocol invariants, and available bounded evidence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-017"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-017 | MUST | applicable | Every Compatibility Assessment report | The report MUST identify its assessed implementation, target, surface, scope, basis, limitations, and bounded result without creating a separate protocol truth or conformance determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-018"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-018 | MUST | applicable | Every compatibility target, surface, statement, profile, declaration, and assessment | Each structure MUST retain its own bounded identity and role and MUST remain distinct from every other compatibility structure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-019"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-019 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every compatibility claim or assessment | Compatibility MUST NOT establish Protocol Succession, Protocol Lineage membership, migration eligibility, generation replacement, or retirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-020"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-020 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every compatibility claim or assessment | Compatibility MUST NOT merge protocol-generation identities or establish complete semantic equivalence between distinct generations or implementations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-021"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-021 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every compatibility structure or assessment | Compatibility MUST NOT be interpreted as an Implementation Profile, complete Protocol Realization, conformance claim, conformance assessment, conformance determination, certification basis, certification decision, certification status, or certification recognition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-022"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-022 | MUST | applicable | Every compatibility consequence of protocol evolution or migration | The consequence MUST preserve RF-04 ownership of succession, lineage, versioning, deprecation, migration, coexistence, and retirement and MUST identify the affected target and surface explicitly. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S13, PE-G4-SOURCE-S14, PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S16, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-023"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-023 | MUST | applicable | Every failed, superseded, withdrawn, or historically retained compatibility statement, declaration, or assessment | Its lifecycle status MUST remain explicit without mutating the identity or semantics of the target generation, implementation, surface, or historical compatibility record. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-compat-024"></a> PE-G4-COMPAT-024 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every compatibility identifier, statement, declaration, assessment, field, diagnostic, test, report, and observability surface | The surface MUST NOT identify a Private Actor, carry enrollment or admission provenance, create reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or compose compatibility metadata into an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | STATE |

## SF-14 / RF-42 — Implementation profiles

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-001"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Implementation Profile | A profile MUST be one uniquely identified, bounded, and complete implementation-facing selection and organization of applicable normative requirements derived from one identified Privacy Engine Protocol Specification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-002"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | Profile identity MUST distinguish the profile from its source specification, other profiles, compatibility profiles, implementations, reference implementations, conformance artifacts, and certifications. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-003"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | The profile MUST identify one bounded implementation target by role, capability set, deployment class, operational responsibility, or another specification-permitted implementation category. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-004"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | The profile MUST identify exactly one source Protocol Specification and the published protocol generation from which that specification derives. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-005"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | The profile MUST declare a precise scope sufficient to resolve the applicability of every normative requirement relevant to its target. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-006"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-006 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement relevant to an Implementation Profile target | The profile MUST record the requirement as applicable, non-applicable under an identified specification rule, conditionally applicable, or resolved through a permitted specification choice. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-007"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Implementation Profile applicability decision | Applicability MUST NOT be invented from preference, convenience, implementation difficulty, existing implementation behavior, deployment topology, or a desired assessment result. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-008"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-008 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement selected by an Implementation Profile | Selection MUST reference the same stable normative requirement defined by the source Protocol Specification and MUST preserve its identity. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-009"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-009 | MUST | applicable | Every applicable requirement in an Implementation Profile | The profile MUST preserve the requirement's complete meaning, operative strength, applicability conditions, prohibitions, permissions, success behavior, and failure behavior unchanged. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-010"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-010 | MAY | applicable | Every choice explicitly permitted by the source Protocol Specification | A profile MAY resolve, constrain, require support for, exclude, or leave the choice implementation-defined only within the choice space permitted by the specification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-011"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-011 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | A profile MUST NOT invent a choice, widen a choice space, treat mandatory behavior as optional, permit prohibited behavior, or omit a mandatory choice that must be resolved at profile level. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-012"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile composition | The composition MUST identify the profile, source specification, target, scope, applicability decisions, permitted-choice resolutions, dependencies, and completeness conditions without duplicating normative requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-013"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-013 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile dependency | The dependency MUST identify the depended-upon profile, direction, applicability condition, and required obligation boundary without merging profile identities or transferring authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-014"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-014 | MUST | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | The profile MUST account for every requirement relevant to its declared scope and MUST fail profile construction when any applicable obligation, applicability decision, required choice, or dependency remains unresolved. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-015"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-015 | MUST | applicable | Every semantically changed Implementation Profile | A change to scope, applicability, dependencies, requirement selection, or resolved choices MUST produce a distinguishable profile version or identity where retaining the prior identity would imply unchanged obligations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-016"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-016 | MAY | applicable | One Protocol Specification | Multiple Implementation Profiles MAY derive from the same specification where each profile has a distinct identity, bounded target, complete applicability resolution, and preserved requirement semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-017"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-017 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | A profile MUST NOT modify, replace, weaken, strengthen, supersede, or become the Protocol Specification or published protocol generation from which it derives. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-018"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-018 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Implementation Profile | A profile MUST NOT be identified with an implementation, Compatibility Profile, conformance assessment, conformance determination, certification basis, certification decision, certification status, or certification recognition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-019"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-019 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every incomplete Protocol Specification | A complete final Implementation Profile MUST NOT be published from an incomplete Protocol Specification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S07, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-020"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-020 | MUST | applicable | Every profile construction or preservation failure | The profile MUST fail rather than invent, repair, weaken, strengthen, merge, divide, reinterpret, or infer a replacement requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-profile-021"></a> PE-G4-PROFILE-021 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every profile identifier, applicability record, choice record, dependency, report, example, diagnostic, and observability surface | The surface MUST NOT identify a Private Actor, create reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or convert implementation-target metadata into an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | STATE |

## SF-14 / RF-43 — Reference implementations

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-001"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Reference Implementation | A Reference Implementation MUST be one identified implementation explicitly designated as an inspectable realization of one identified Implementation Profile within one declared reference scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-002"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Reference Implementation | Reference-implementation identity MUST distinguish the implementation, version, realized profile, reference scope, and reference status from every other reference realization. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-003"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-003 | MUST | applicable | Every reference designation | Reference status MUST be explicit, bounded to one identified implementation-profile relationship and scope, and independently reviewable. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-004"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-004 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every reference designation | Designation MUST NOT be interpreted as protocol publication, protocol authority, automatic conformance, certification, compatibility, defect freedom, or exclusive implementation status. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S12, PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-005"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Reference Implementation | The declared reference scope MUST identify exactly which profile obligations, capability set, version, and implementation boundary the reference status covers. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-006"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Reference Implementation realizing an Implementation Profile | The implementation MUST realize every applicable profile requirement within its declared reference scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-007"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Reference Implementation | The implementation MUST preserve the applicable profile's required success behavior, failure behavior, privacy constraints, identity separations, and authority boundaries. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-008"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-008 | MAY | applicable | Every Reference Implementation | A Reference Implementation MAY select one implementation choice where that choice is explicitly permitted by the applicable profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-009"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-009 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every implementation choice selected by a Reference Implementation | The selected choice MUST NOT become a universal requirement, profile amendment, specification amendment, or prohibition against another permitted realization. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-010"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-010 | MAY | applicable | One Implementation Profile | Multiple Reference Implementations MAY realize the same profile where each independently satisfies every applicable obligation within its declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-011"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-011 | MAY | applicable | One implementation realizing multiple Implementation Profiles | The implementation MAY hold separate reference status for multiple profiles only where each profile relationship, version, scope, dependencies, and designation remain explicitly and independently identified. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-012"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-012 | MUST | applicable | Every materially changed Reference Implementation version | A materially changed version MUST remain separately identifiable where the change affects profile realization, permitted-choice resolution, normative behavior, interoperability behavior, defect status, or reference scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-013"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-013 | MUST | applicable | Every maintained Reference Implementation version | The version MUST remain sufficiently stable, available, and inspectable to support its declared reference uses without silently rewriting its identified historical behavior. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-014"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-014 | MUST | applicable | Every detected Reference Implementation defect or divergence | The defect or divergence MUST be evaluated against the applicable profile without redefining, weakening, strengthening, or repairing the profile requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-015"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-015 | MUST | applicable | Every Reference Implementation unable to preserve an applicable obligation | The implementation MUST fail explicitly, and its implementation or designation MUST be corrected, narrowed, suspended, withdrawn, or superseded as appropriate. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-016"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-016 | MUST | applicable | Every ordinary implementation compared with a Reference Implementation | The ordinary implementation MUST be evaluated against its applicable profile rather than against incidental structure or behavior of the Reference Implementation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-017"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-017 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every ordinary implementation | An ordinary implementation MUST NOT be required to copy reference source structure, language, algorithms, storage, deployment topology, APIs, logging, timing, or other behavior not required by the applicable profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-018"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-018 | MAY | applicable | Every conformance, certification, compatibility, testing, or interoperability activity | The activity MAY use a Reference Implementation as supporting evidence, comparison behavior, test infrastructure, fixture, peer, or implementation example. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-019"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-019 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every conformance, certification, or compatibility activity using a Reference Implementation | Agreement, interoperability, code reuse, designation, or behavioral similarity MUST NOT replace the applicable conformance basis, certification basis, or bounded compatibility assessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-020"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-020 | MUST | applicable | Every disagreement between a Reference Implementation and an upstream requirement | The disagreement MUST be resolved against the applicable Implementation Profile, Protocol Specification, and published protocol generation in that order of derivation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-refimpl-021"></a> PE-G4-REFIMPL-021 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every reference identifier, designation, implementation record, version, test, diagnostic, report, log, and observability surface | The surface MUST NOT identify a Private Actor, create reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or compose reference-implementation metadata into an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | STATE |

## SF-15 / RF-44 — Published conformance requirements and requirement sets

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-001"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Conformance Requirement | The requirement MUST be one bounded assessable expression of one existing applicable normative requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-002"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Conformance Requirement | The requirement MUST possess a stable identity distinct from its source normative requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-003"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Conformance Requirement | The requirement MUST identify its source normative requirement, Protocol Specification, and published protocol generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-004"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-004 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance derivation | Derivation MUST preserve source-requirement identity, complete normative meaning, and source traceability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-005"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-005 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance derivation | Derivation MUST preserve operative strength, applicability conditions, and permitted-choice boundaries. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-006"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Published Conformance Requirement | The requirement MUST NOT create, remove, weaken, strengthen, repair, reinterpret, or replace a protocol obligation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-007"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-007 | MUST | applicable | Every published Conformance Requirement | Publication MUST make identity, source, meaning, strength, applicability, version, and traceability independently reviewable. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-008"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-008 | MUST | applicable | Every published Conformance Requirement | The published requirement MUST remain immutable; semantic change MUST create a distinguishable requirement. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-009"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-009 | MUST | applicable | Every Published Conformance Requirement Set | The set MUST be one identified, bounded, immutable collection of applicable Published Conformance Requirements. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S58 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-010"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-010 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Requirement Set | Set identity MUST distinguish its target, scope, version, composition, and source generation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S58 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-011"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-011 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Requirement Set | The set MUST identify exactly one bounded assessment target and one declared assessment scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S58 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-012"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-012 | MUST | applicable | Every complete Conformance Requirement Set | The set MUST contain every and only Published Conformance Requirements applicable within its declared target and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S58 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-013"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-013 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Requirement Set constituent | Composition MUST preserve constituent identity, meaning, strength, applicability, ordering where relevant, and traceability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S06, PE-G4-SOURCE-S58 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confreq-014"></a> PE-G4-CONFREQ-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Requirement Set | The set MUST NOT evaluate an implementation, become an Implementation Profile, create obligations, or acquire protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S58 | NONE |

## SF-15 / RF-45 — Conformance artifacts, evidence, and packages

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-001"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Artifact | An artifact MUST be one bounded, immutable, referenceable unit documenting objective implementation information relevant to conformance. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-002"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Artifact | Artifact identity MUST distinguish the artifact, implementation, version, assessment scope, and recorded information. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-003"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Artifact | The artifact MUST preserve integrity, provenance, authorship or production context, and reference stability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-004"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Artifact | Artifact content MUST remain objective, reviewable, and attributable to its declared implementation boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-005"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-005 | MUST | applicable | Every artifact used as evidence | Use as evidence MUST preserve artifact identity and MUST record the evidentiary relationship separately. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-006"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Artifact | An artifact MUST NOT establish correctness, conformance, certification, protocol truth, or protocol authority merely by existing. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-007"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Evidence set | Evidence MUST be one identified, published, complete, and traceable support set for one claim and declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S60 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-008"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Evidence set | Evidence identity MUST distinguish its claim, implementation, profile, requirement set, scope, version, and constituent artifacts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S60 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-009"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-009 | MUST | applicable | Every evidence constituent | Evidence MUST trace each supported claim element to applicable requirements and preserved artifacts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S57, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-010"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-010 | MUST | applicable | Every complete Conformance Evidence set | Evidence MUST account for every applicable conformance requirement within its declared evidentiary scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S58, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-011"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-011 | MAY | applicable | Every Conformance Evidence set | Evidence MAY compose testing, inspection, analysis, review, measurement, and preserved artifacts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-012"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-012 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Evidence set | Evidence MUST NOT perform assessment, determine conformance, establish certification, or become protocol truth. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-013"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-013 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Package | A package MUST be one canonical, identified, immutable publication unit for one bounded conformance assessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S61 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-014"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-014 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Package | Package identity MUST distinguish its implementation, profile, claim, scope, version, evidence, artifacts, and publication. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S61 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-015"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-015 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Package | The package MUST identify its artifacts, evidence, claim references, applicable requirement set, scope, and traceability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S58, PE-G4-SOURCE-S61 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-016"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-016 | MUST | applicable | Every published Conformance Package | The package MUST remain immutable; changed composition MUST create a distinguishable package. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S61 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-017"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-017 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Package constituent | Packaging MUST preserve constituent identity, content, integrity, provenance, scope, and independent meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S61 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-018"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-018 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Package | The package MUST NOT merge constituents, perform assessment, or establish conformance by publication. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S61, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-confevid-019"></a> PE-G4-CONFEVID-019 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Package | The package MUST NOT constitute certification or replace a Certification Basis, Decision, Status, or recognition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S61, PE-G4-SOURCE-S65, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S67, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |

## SF-15 / RF-46 — Conformance claims, assessments, and determinations

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-001"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Claim | A claim MUST be one bounded assertion that one identified implementation satisfies one identified Implementation Profile within one declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S62, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-002"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Claim | Claim identity MUST distinguish the assertion, implementation, profile, specification, generation, scope, and version. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S62, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-003"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Claim | The claim MUST identify its implementation, implementation version where relevant, profile, specification, generation, and assessment scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S62 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-004"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Claim | The claim MUST remain bounded to its declared implementation-profile relationship and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S62 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-005"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-005 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Claim | The claim MUST NOT establish Protocol Realization or replace implementation obligations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S62 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-006"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Conformance Claim | The claim MUST NOT perform assessment, produce a determination, establish conformance, or establish certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S62, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-007"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Assessment | An assessment MUST be one identified bounded evaluation of one identified claim concerning one implementation, profile, and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-008"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Assessment | Assessment identity MUST distinguish the evaluation occurrence, claim, implementation, profile, requirement set, evidence, and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-009"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-009 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Assessment | The assessment MUST declare the exact target, applicable requirement set, evidence boundary, and assessment scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S58, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-010"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-010 | MUST | applicable | Every complete Conformance Assessment | The assessment MUST evaluate every applicable requirement within its declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S58, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-011"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-011 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Assessment | The assessment MUST use valid, applicable, integrity-preserved, and traceable evidence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S59, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-012"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Assessment | The assessment MUST evaluate the claim without modifying or becoming the claim. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S62, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-013"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-013 | MUST | applicable | Every completed Conformance Assessment | Completion MUST produce at most one distinct Conformance Determination for that assessment occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-014"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-014 | MUST | applicable | Every assessment unable to reach a supported classification | The assessment MUST fail or remain indeterminate without producing a conformant or non-conformant determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-015"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-015 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Determination | A determination MUST be the bounded authoritative result of one completed Conformance Assessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-016"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-016 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Determination | Determination identity MUST distinguish its assessment, implementation, profile, scope, requirement set, and classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-017"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-017 | MUST | applicable | Every conformant determination | The determination MUST record satisfaction of every applicable requirement within its declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-018"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-018 | MUST | applicable | Every non-conformant determination | The determination MUST record that at least one applicable requirement within scope was not satisfied. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-019"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-019 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Determination | The determination MUST record exactly one classification: conformant within scope or non-conformant within scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-020"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-020 | MUST | applicable | Every Conformance Determination | The determination MUST identify its complete bounded scope; no unstated scope is implied. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-021"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-021 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every partial Conformance Assessment | Partial assessment MUST NOT establish complete conformance beyond the requirements actually assessed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-022"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-022 | MUST | applicable | Every published Conformance Determination | The historical determination MUST remain immutable and independently identifiable. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-023"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-023 | MUST | applicable | Every correction affecting previous conformance | Correction MUST be evaluated through a new assessment producing a new determination where completed. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-024"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-024 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance or compatibility activity | Conformance satisfaction and compatibility relationships MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-025"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-025 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance or certification activity | Conformance determination and certification recognition MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-026"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-026 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance authority | Conformance authority MUST remain bounded assessment authority within the declared target and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-conform-027"></a> PE-G4-CONFORM-027 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every claim, assessment, determination, evidence object, report, test, diagnostic, identifier, and metadata surface | The surface MUST NOT identify a Private Actor, create reusable participant identity, create cross-action linkage, or create an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | STATE |

## SF-15 / RF-47 — Certification bases, decisions, and statuses

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-001"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Basis | A basis MUST be one complete, immutable, identified evaluation basis for one bounded certification decision. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S65 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-002"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Basis | Basis identity MUST distinguish its subject, target, scope, requirements, packages, determinations, policy, and version. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S65 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-003"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-003 | MUST | applicable | Every complete Certification Basis | The basis MUST identify every criterion and supporting conformance structure required for its declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S57, PE-G4-SOURCE-S58, PE-G4-SOURCE-S61, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S65 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-004"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-004 | MUST | applicable | Every published Certification Basis | The basis MUST remain immutable; changed criteria or composition MUST create a distinguishable basis. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S15, PE-G4-SOURCE-S65 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-005"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Basis | The basis MUST declare one bounded certification subject, target, program context, and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S65, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-006"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-006 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Certification Basis | The basis MUST NOT define its decision result, modify conformance, or create protocol obligations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S65 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-007"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-007 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Decision | A decision MUST be one identified immutable pass-or-fail evaluation of one subject and package against one basis. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-008"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-008 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Decision | Decision identity MUST distinguish the subject, package, basis, scope, decision occurrence, and classification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-009"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-009 | MUST | applicable | Every passing Certification Decision | Pass MUST record that the identified subject and package satisfy the identified basis within scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-010"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-010 | MUST | applicable | Every failing Certification Decision | Fail MUST record that the identified subject or package does not satisfy the identified basis within scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-011"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-011 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Decision | The decision MUST record exactly one classification: pass or fail. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-012"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Decision | The decision MUST identify the exact Certification Basis against which it was made. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S65, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-013"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-013 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Certification Decision | The decision MUST NOT perform conformance assessment, alter evidence, repair failure, or modify a Conformance Determination. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-014"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-014 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Status | Status MUST be one recorded lifecycle state of one identified certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S67, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-015"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-015 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Status | Status identity MUST distinguish the certification, lifecycle occurrence, effective state, authority, and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S67, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-016"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-016 | MUST | applicable | Every certification lifecycle change | The change MUST occur through a defined issuance, maintenance, suspension, withdrawal, expiry, or reassessment event. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S67, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-017"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-017 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification Status | Status MUST remain distinct from its supporting Certification Decision and historical decisions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S67 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-018"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-018 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Certification Status | Status MUST NOT become implementation state, conformance truth, protocol truth, or protocol authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S67 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-certdet-019"></a> PE-G4-CERTDET-019 | MUST | applicable | Every certification lifecycle update | The update MUST preserve historical bases, decisions, determinations, and prior status occurrences unchanged. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S65, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S67 | NONE |

## SF-15 / RF-48 — Certification recognition, scope, and authority

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-001"></a> PE-G4-CERT-001 | MUST | applicable | Every Privacy Engine Certification | Certification MUST be one bounded recognition of one identified implementation with respect to one identified Implementation Profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-002"></a> PE-G4-CERT-002 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | Certification identity MUST distinguish the recognition, implementation, profile, scope, basis, decisions, determinations, authority, and status. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-003"></a> PE-G4-CERT-003 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | The certification subject MUST be exactly one identified implementation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-004"></a> PE-G4-CERT-004 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | The certification target MUST be exactly one identified Implementation Profile. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-005"></a> PE-G4-CERT-005 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | Certification MUST apply only within its explicitly declared implementation, profile, determination, and recognition scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-006"></a> PE-G4-CERT-006 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | Certification MUST be supported by one or more identified conformant Conformance Determinations covering its declared scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-007"></a> PE-G4-CERT-007 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every complete-certification claim | Complete certification MUST NOT be claimed unless supporting determinations cover every applicable profile requirement within the declared complete scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-008"></a> PE-G4-CERT-008 | MUST | applicable | Every partial Certification | Partial recognition MUST explicitly identify its bounded scope and MUST remain distinguishable from complete certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-009"></a> PE-G4-CERT-009 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | The issuing certification authority MUST possess a stable identity distinct from the certification and assessed implementation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-010"></a> PE-G4-CERT-010 | MUST | applicable | Every certification authority | The authority MUST declare the certification program, responsibilities, recognition boundary, and scope within which it acts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-011"></a> PE-G4-CERT-011 | MUST | applicable | Every certification authority action | Issuance, maintenance, suspension, withdrawal, and update authority MUST remain bounded by the declared program and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-012"></a> PE-G4-CERT-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Certification | Recognition MUST remain distinct from conformance assessment without replacing it. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-013"></a> PE-G4-CERT-013 | MUST | applicable | Every certification or compatibility statement | Certification recognition and compatibility MUST remain distinct. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-014"></a> PE-G4-CERT-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every Certification and certification authority | Neither certification nor its authority MUST become protocol authority or redefine upstream semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S10, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-015"></a> PE-G4-CERT-015 | MUST | applicable | Every certification withdrawal | Withdrawal MUST change certification status only and MUST preserve implementation, profile, determination, decision, and protocol history. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S67, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-016"></a> PE-G4-CERT-016 | MUST | applicable | Every certification reassessment | Reassessment MUST use new identified evidence, assessment, determination, basis, or decision occurrences where applicable. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S65, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-017"></a> PE-G4-CERT-017 | MUST | applicable | Every certification lifecycle | Historical Conformance Determinations and Certification Decisions MUST remain immutable through issuance, withdrawal, correction, and reassessment. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-018"></a> PE-G4-CERT-018 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance and certification structure | Public identifiers and records MUST preserve Private Actor separation and action-bounded unlinkability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-019"></a> PE-G4-CERT-019 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance and certification naming surface | Names, fields, routes, storage keys, logs, diagnostics, examples, tests, and reports MUST use neutral non-correlation-bearing terminology. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-cert-020"></a> PE-G4-CERT-020 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every conformance and certification composition | Composition MUST NOT expose enrollment provenance, wallet identity, reusable participant identity, cross-action linkage, or an actor-reconstruction bridge. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | STATE |

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-001"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-001 | MUST | applicable | Every downstream Privacy Engine layer | The layer MUST preserve applicable upstream protocol semantics within its declared role and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-002"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-002 | MUST | applicable | The complete downstream chain | The chain MUST remain Generation to Specification to Profile to Implementation to Conformance to Certification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S03, PE-G4-SOURCE-S08, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-003"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-003 | MUST | applicable | Every selected semantic subject or obligation | Selection and every later layer MUST preserve the same selected subject or obligation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-004"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-004 | MUST | applicable | Every normative requirement crossing a downstream boundary | The boundary MUST preserve stable requirement identity and traceability. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S57 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-005"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-005 | MUST | applicable | Every semantic subject crossing a downstream boundary | The boundary MUST preserve complete normative meaning. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-006"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-006 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement crossing a downstream boundary | The boundary MUST preserve operative strength unchanged. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S04, PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-007"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-007 | MUST | applicable | Every requirement crossing a downstream boundary | The boundary MUST preserve applicability and its governing conditions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-008"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-008 | MUST | applicable | Every permitted protocol choice | Every downstream constraint or selection MUST remain within the published choice boundary. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-009"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-009 | MUST | applicable | Every protocol subject and occurrence | Downstream processing MUST preserve subject identity, occurrence identity, and established identity separation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-010"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-010 | MUST | applicable | Every downstream representation and surface | The representation or surface MUST preserve privacy, unlinkability, minimum disclosure, and Private Actor separation. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20 | STATE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-011"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-011 | MUST | applicable | Every proof, public input, verification, and validity boundary | The boundary MUST preserve declared proof meaning, public-input meaning, verification meaning, and complete validity conditions. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-012"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-012 | MUST | applicable | Every Protocol Action use chain | The chain MUST preserve Action, Evaluation, Determination, acceptance, and rejection identities and semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S24, PE-G4-SOURCE-S25 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-013"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-013 | MUST | applicable | Every transition use chain | The chain MUST preserve mapping, occurrence, authorization, execution, commitment, state, history, consequence, and failure semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S26, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-014"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-014 | MUST | applicable | Every application or adapter boundary | Application representation and adapter translation MUST preserve selected protocol meaning or fail. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S29, PE-G4-SOURCE-S40, PE-G4-SOURCE-S41, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42, PE-G4-SOURCE-S44 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-015"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-015 | MUST | applicable | Every runtime or implementation boundary | Operational responsibility and implementation realization MUST preserve semantics without acquiring semantic ownership. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S45, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-016"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-016 | MUST | applicable | Every compatibility structure | Compatibility MUST preserve its declared target, surface, relationship, scope, and separation from upstream semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S48, PE-G4-SOURCE-S49, PE-G4-SOURCE-S50, PE-G4-SOURCE-S51, PE-G4-SOURCE-S52, PE-G4-SOURCE-S53, PE-G4-SOURCE-S54 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-017"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-017 | MUST | applicable | Every profile and Reference Implementation boundary | Profile selection and reference realization MUST preserve applicable requirements and permitted-choice boundaries. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S56 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-018"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-018 | MUST | applicable | Every conformance boundary | Requirements, evidence, assessment, and determination MUST preserve upstream meaning and bounded scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S57, PE-G4-SOURCE-S58, PE-G4-SOURCE-S59, PE-G4-SOURCE-S60, PE-G4-SOURCE-S61, PE-G4-SOURCE-S62, PE-G4-SOURCE-S63, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-019"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-019 | MUST | applicable | Every certification boundary | Basis, decision, status, recognition, and authority MUST preserve supporting conformance meaning and scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S65, PE-G4-SOURCE-S66, PE-G4-SOURCE-S67, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-020"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-020 | MUST | applicable | Every successful downstream operation | Success MUST preserve the selected subject or obligation without substitution. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-021"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-021 | MUST | applicable | Every downstream operation unable to demonstrate preservation | The operation MUST fail, remain unsupported, narrow, withdraw, or be superseded under its defined lifecycle. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-022"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-022 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every preservation failure | Failure MUST NOT repair, reinterpret, replace, or synthesize missing semantics. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-023"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-023 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream layer | The layer MUST NOT substitute a different subject, occurrence, requirement, determination, transition, or recognition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-024"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-024 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream layer | The layer MUST NOT infer an absent obligation, permission, identity, evidence item, classification, or authority. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S46 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-025"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-025 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream layer | The layer MUST NOT silently widen or narrow identity, meaning, applicability, choice, target, or scope. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S55, PE-G4-SOURCE-S64, PE-G4-SOURCE-S68 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-026"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-026 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every downstream composition | Composition MUST NOT merge distinct subjects or divide one subject into replacement identities or obligations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S05, PE-G4-SOURCE-S17, PE-G4-SOURCE-S18 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-sempres-027"></a> PE-G4-SEMPRES-027 | MUST | applicable | Every Evaluation, Determination, failure, retry, realization, diagnostic and observability surface | Every Evaluation, Determination, failure, retry, representation, publication, transport, delivery, diagnostic and observability surface MUST preserve Private Actor non-disclosure, neutral naming, action-bounded public identity and the prohibition on reusable participant identity, actor provenance and cross-action linkage. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S25, PE-G4-SOURCE-S27, PE-G4-SOURCE-S30, PE-G4-SOURCE-S32, PE-G4-SOURCE-S42, PE-G4-SOURCE-S43, PE-G4-SOURCE-S47 | PE-G3-TSE-SEMANTIC-DIFFERENCE-001 |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepres-001"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRES-001 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Every comparison, recovery and commitment MUST preserve the distinct identities and meanings of E, D, authorization, T, U, Q0 and successor state. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepres-002-a01"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRES-002-A01 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Predecessor and successor generations MUST coexist. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepres-002-a02"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRES-002-A02 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Any future succession MUST remain directional and non-retroactive. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69, PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |
| <a id="pe-g4-tsepres-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEPRES-003 | MUST | applicable | ALWAYS | Candidate acceptance, specification acceptance, Protocol Publication, succession and Protocol Specification Publication MUST remain separate acts. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S69 | NONE |

| <a id="pe-g4-exclude-014"></a> PE-G4-EXCLUDE-014 | MUST NOT | applicable | Every protocol, assessment, and certification surface | The surface MUST NOT create cross-action linkage or extend proof meaning into actor identification. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S18, PE-G4-SOURCE-S20 | STATE |

| <a id="pe-g4-tseexclude-003"></a> PE-G4-TSEEXCLUDE-003 | MUST NOT | applicable | ALWAYS | A failed U MUST NOT be repaired, resumed, reactivated, reinterpreted or converted into a committed occurrence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S72 | NONE |

## RF-11 — approved observation-aware procedural delta

These five homes supplement the original 926 candidate homes; total 931. This is unpublished construction, not conformance admission.

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-066"></a> PE-G4-CAP-066 | MUST | conditional | G4 profiles subject to the approved observation-aware privacy construction | The G4 privacy construction MUST include every declared program field, root argument, component side/status, ReadObject metadata cell, operation, expression branch, parameter/call/control relationship, governing owning schema, public PI alias and source-required producer observation/custody path; absent source completeness MUST remain an admission failure. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S83#universe | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#privacy |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-067"></a> PE-G4-CAP-067 | MUST | conditional | G4 profiles subject to the approved observation-aware privacy construction | Every required observer and coalition visibility assessment MUST be independently source-established for the exact regenerated graph; missing or unknown visibility MUST fail, and a caller-supplied completeness flag MUST NOT substitute for that evidence. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S83#visibility | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#privacy |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-068"></a> PE-G4-CAP-068 | MUST | conditional | G4 profiles subject to the approved observation-aware privacy construction | Cross-context correlation assessment MUST use the independently established observable graph for each required observer coalition, preserve the existing permitted constant-equality exception only in that assessment, and include equality of values visible to that coalition. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S83#correlation | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#privacy |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-069"></a> PE-G4-CAP-069 | MUST | conditional | G4 profiles subject to the approved observation-aware privacy construction | Actor-reconstruction assessment MUST retain the complete regenerated graph, including confidential, non-observed and permitted constant-equality edges, without visibility filtering. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S83#actor | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#privacy |
| <a id="pe-g4-cap-070"></a> PE-G4-CAP-070 | MUST NOT | conditional | G4 profiles subject to the approved observation-aware privacy construction | The observation-aware correction MUST NOT introduce public-linkage exceptions, suppress existing public PI, convert changing roots or action-local values into generation constants, or waive the unchanged public uniqueness and actor-protection obligations. | PE-G4-SOURCE-S83#no-exceptions | PE-G4-SOURCE-S70, PE-G4-SOURCE-S80#privacy |

## RF-11 — approved bounded public relationships

Five source-scoped homes supplement the original 931; current total 936. S84 specializes S83's no-exceptions rule only as explicitly approved. No publication or conformance admission is asserted.

| Requirement | Strength | Applicability | Scope | Normative text | Owner source | Supporting references |
